【目录】
  1. index.html        页面入口
  2. css/app.css       全部样式（999 行）
  3. js/project.js     项目配置 / 素材注册表
  4. js/app.js         主逻辑（2800+ 行，四个 Tab）
  5. server.js         Node 后端（静态托管 + 万擎 LLM SSE 代理）
  6. data/story.json   荒年记全剧本（5 章 230 分镜 38 分叉）
  7. data/trivia.json  等待期冷知识文案库

【运行】cd demo-platform && node server.js  →  http://127.0.0.1:8899

【依赖】/api/gen 全链路 AI 生成需 Node 后端打万擎：
        wanqing-api.corp.kuaishou.com/api/gateway/v1/messages
        KEY 在 server.js 顶部 WQ_KEY 常量
        素材 assets/ 232 文件 9.1MB、runtime/ 真机包 8.2MB 未含在本 txt

 * ======================================================================= */
let S = null;                 // story.json
const state = {
  view: 'create',             // create | history | editor
  step: 'story',              // story | element | shot | publish
  inputOpen: false, // 故事页「输入·生成参数」默认折叠（只读，不可修改）
  docOpen: true,  // 故事页「输出剧本」默认展开

  flowTool: 'pan',            // pan（默认·小手拖拽预览）| select（点击编辑）
  flowPos: {},                // 流程模式拖拽后的节点坐标（按章节）
  chapter: 'EP01',
  epOpen: { EP01: true },     // 侧栏剧集树：各集独立折叠态（与「当前章节」解耦）
  theme: 'modern',
  // 发布页状态：合成闸门 + 双 Tab（测试预览 / 上架）+ 历史

    tab: 'test',            // test | release | history

/* ---------- 平台 toast ---------- */
const MOCK_TIP = '我是假的 等研发爸爸们做';
let _ptT = null;
function toast(msg = MOCK_TIP) {
  let n = $('#ptoast');
  if (!n) { n = el('div', 'ptoast'); n.id = 'ptoast'; document.body.appendChild(n); }
  n.textContent = msg;
  n.style.animation = 'none'; void n.offsetWidth; n.style.animation = '';
  clearTimeout(_ptT);
  _ptT = setTimeout(() => { n.remove(); }, 1800);
}
/** 全局事件委托：凡带 data-mock 的元素（重新生成 / 一键重生 / 新增 / 编辑 / 升级 …）一律弹 toast */
document.addEventListener('click', ev => {
  const t = ev.target.closest('[data-mock]');
  if (!t) return;
  ev.preventDefault(); ev.stopPropagation();
  toast(t.dataset.mock || MOCK_TIP);
}, true);
const $ = (s, r = document) => r.querySelector(s);
const el = (tag, cls, html) => { const n = document.createElement(tag); if (cls) n.className = cls; if (html != null) n.innerHTML = html; return n; };
const esc = s => String(s ?? '').replace(/[&<>]/g, c => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;' }[c]));

/* ---------- 媒体路径 ---------- */
function mediaUrl(p) {
  if (!p) return '';
  if (/^https?:/.test(p)) return p;
  return 'assets/' + p.replace(/^\.?\//, '');
}
function nodePoster(n) {
  const m = n && n.media; if (!m) return '';
  if (m.poster) return mediaUrl(m.poster);
  if (m.kind === 'image') return mediaUrl(m.file);
  return '';
}
/** 无自有素材的对白节点沿用上游画面（与真机播放一致）：预计算继承表 */
let INHERIT = {};
function buildInherit() {
  INHERIT = {};
  const pred = {};
  Object.keys(S.nodes).forEach(k => {
    const n = S.nodes[k];
    const outs = n.type === 'choice' ? (n.options || []).map(o => o.next) : [n.next];
    outs.forEach(t => { if (t && !(t in pred)) pred[t] = k; });
  });
  const resolve = (id, guard) => {
    if (!id || !S.nodes[id] || guard > 40) return '';
    const p = nodePoster(S.nodes[id]);
    if (p) return p;
    return resolve(pred[id], guard + 1);
  };
  Object.keys(S.nodes).forEach(k => {
    if (!nodePoster(S.nodes[k])) INHERIT[k] = resolve(pred[k], 0);
  });
  // 兜底：孤儿节点（无前驱）用同 scene 的第一张画面，保证预览与列表不空
  const byScene = {};
  Object.keys(S.nodes).forEach(k => {
    const n = S.nodes[k], p = nodePoster(n);
    if (p && n.scene && !byScene[n.scene]) byScene[n.scene] = p;
  });
  Object.keys(S.nodes).forEach(k => {
    if (!nodePoster(S.nodes[k]) && !INHERIT[k]) INHERIT[k] = byScene[S.nodes[k].scene] || '';
  });
}
/** 展示用画面：自有 → 继承 */
function frameOf(id) { const n = S.nodes[id]; return nodePoster(n) || INHERIT[id] || ''; }

/* ---------- 章节 / 节点索引 ---------- */
function chapterOf(id) { const n = S.nodes[id]; return (n && n.ep) || 'EP01'; }
function nodesOfChapter(ep) { return Object.keys(S.nodes).filter(k => chapterOf(k) === ep); }
/** 从章节 start 做 BFS，得到有序节点（分镜列表按此排） */
function orderedNodes(ep) {
  const chap = S.chapters.find(c => c.id === ep);
  const seen = new Set(), out = [];
  const q = chap ? [chap.start] : [];
  while (q.length) {
    const id = q.shift(); if (!id || seen.has(id) || !S.nodes[id]) continue;
    seen.add(id);
    if (chapterOf(id) === ep) out.push(id);
    const n = S.nodes[id];
    (n.type === 'choice' ? (n.options || []).map(o => o.next) : [n.next]).forEach(x => x && q.push(x));
  }
  // 补上未被 BFS 命中的同章节点（跨章入口 / 孤立结局）
  nodesOfChapter(ep).forEach(k => { if (!seen.has(k)) out.push(k); });
  return out;
}
function transKind(n) {
  if (n.type === 'end') return { k: 'end', label: '终止', cls: 'c-end', icon: '■' };
  if (n.type === 'choice') {
    const ad = (n.options || []).some(o => o.ad);
    return ad ? { k: 'ad', label: '选项 · 含广告解锁', cls: 'c-ad', icon: '◆' }
      : { k: 'choice', label: '选项', cls: 'c-choice', icon: '◆' };
  }
  return { k: 'nat', label: '自然', cls: '', icon: '─○' };
}
function statLabel(st) {
  if (!st) return '';
  const map = { wei: '威望', qing: '爱意', liang: '存粮' };
  return Object.entries(st).filter(([, v]) => Math.abs(v) < 90)
    .map(([k, v]) => `${map[k] || k}${v > 0 ? '+' : ''}${v}`).join(' ');
}
function isDeadOpt(o) { return Object.values(o.stat || {}).some(v => v <= -90); }

/* ---------- 全局统计 ---------- */
function stats() {
  const ks = Object.keys(S.nodes);
  let choice = 0, end = 0, ad = 0, withMedia = 0, video = 0, framed = 0;
  ks.forEach(k => {
    const n = S.nodes[k];
    if (n.type === 'choice') { choice++; if ((n.options || []).some(o => o.ad)) ad++; }
    if (n.type === 'end') end++;
    const m = n.media; if (m && (m.file || m.poster)) withMedia++;
    if (m && m.kind === 'video') video++;
    if (frameOf(k)) framed++;
  });
  return { total: ks.length, choice, end, ad, withMedia, framed, video, chapters: S.chapters.length, endings: Object.keys(S.endings).length };
}

/* =======================================================================
 * 外壳
 * ======================================================================= */
function renderShell() {
  const st = stats();
  if (state.view === 'play') { renderPlay(); return; }
  const isEd = state.view === 'editor';
  const isDraft = isEd && state.src === 'draft' && DRAFTS.cur && DRAFTS.cur.story;
  $('#app').className = isEd ? 'ed' : '';
  $('#app').innerHTML = `
  <div class="rail" id="rail">
    <div class="rail-in">
      <div class="logo" data-go="create" title="返回首页"><img src="assets/brand/icon.png" alt=""></div>
      <button class="ri ${state.view === 'create' ? 'on' : ''}" data-go="create">
        <span class="ic">＋</span><span class="lb">新增</span></button>
      <button class="ri ${state.view === 'history' || isEd ? 'on' : ''}" data-go="history">
        <span class="ic">🗂</span><span class="lb">历史项目</span></button>
      <div class="sp"></div>
      <div class="rail-credit"><span class="fi">🔥</span><b>1280</b><span class="up" data-mock>升级</span></div>
      <div class="acct" id="acct">
        <button class="ri me-row" id="meBtn">
          <span class="ic"><i class="me">李</i></span><span class="lb">李慧</span></button>
        <div class="pop hide" id="mePop">
          <div class="who"><b>李慧</b><span>lihui26@kuaishou.com</span></div>
          <button class="out" id="logoutBtn">退出登录</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="main">
    <div class="topbar ${isEd ? '' : 'bare'}">
      ${isEd ? `
        <button class="btn sm" data-go="history">←</button>
        <div class="pname"><span class="dot"></span>${esc(isDraft ? DRAFTS.cur.story.title : PROJECT.title)}${isDraft ? '<span class="dbadge dft">草稿</span>' : ''}</div>
        <span class="ver">${isDraft ? '仅剧本 · 真实模型生成' : esc(S.version) + ' · ' + st.total + ' 节点'}</span>
        <div class="steps">
          ${[['story', '故事'], ['element', '元素'], ['shot', '分镜'], ['publish', '发布']]
      .map(([k, t], i) => `<button data-step="${k}" class="${state.step === k ? 'on' : ''}"><span class="num">${i + 1}</span>${t}</button>`).join('')}
        </div>
        ${state.step === 'publish'
      ? `<span class="tb-st ${state.pub.synthOk ? 'ok' : ''}">${state.pub.synthOk ? '✓ 已合成 · 可分发' : '○ 待合成'}</span>`
      : '<button class="btn pri shine" id="nextStep">下一步 →</button>'}
      ` : ''}
    </div>
    <div class="body">
      <div class="stage" id="stage"></div>
    </div>
  </div>`;
  $('#app').querySelectorAll('[data-go]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.view = b.dataset.go; if (b.dataset.go !== 'editor') state.src = ''; renderShell(); });
  $('#app').querySelectorAll('[data-step]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
    if (isDraft && b.dataset.step !== 'story') { toast('草稿目前只生成了剧本，元素 / 分镜待生成'); return; }
    state.step = b.dataset.step; renderShell();
  });
  const ns = $('#nextStep');
  if (ns) ns.onclick = () => {
    const order = ['story', 'element', 'shot', 'publish'];
    state.step = order[Math.min(order.indexOf(state.step) + 1, 3)];
    renderShell();
  };
  if (state.view === 'create') renderCreate();
  else if (state.view === 'history') renderHistoryList();
  else {
    ({ story: renderStory, element: renderElement, shot: renderShot, publish: renderPublish }[state.step])();
  }
  bindAcct();
}

/* 底部个人头像 → 点击弹出「退出登录」 */
function bindAcct() {
  const btn = $('#meBtn'), pop = $('#mePop');
  if (!btn || !pop) return;
  btn.onclick = ev => { ev.stopPropagation(); pop.classList.toggle('hide'); };
  if (!bindAcct._doc) { bindAcct._doc = true; document.addEventListener('click', () => { const p2 = $('#mePop'); if (p2) p2.classList.add('hide'); }); }
  $('#logoutBtn').onclick = () => { pop.classList.add('hide'); renderLogout(); };
}
function renderLogout() {
  $('#app').innerHTML = `<div class="logout">
    <div class="lo-card">
      <div class="lo-icon"><img src="assets/brand/icon.png" alt=""></div>
      <h2>已退出登录</h2>
      <p>感谢使用 · 快手 AIGC 剧改游创编平台</p>
      <button class="btn pri" id="reLogin">重新登录</button>
    </div>
  </div>`;
  $('#reLogin').onclick = () => { state.view = 'create'; renderShell(); };
}

const IDEAS = [

/* ---------- 预设基础信息：标签组（首项均为「智能推荐」= 不填走默认） ---------- */
const PRESET_GROUPS = [
  ['setting', '游戏设定', ['智能推荐', '逆袭', '复仇', '打脸虐渣', '解密', '豪门恩怨', '追妻', '权谋', '寻亲', '家庭伦理', '先婚后爱', '暗恋成真', '虐恋']],
  ['bg', '游戏背景', ['智能推荐', '都市', '家庭', '豪门', '宫廷', '职场', '农村', '江湖', '仙魔', '校园', '战场', '末世']],
  ['theme', '游戏主题', ['智能推荐', '玄幻仙侠', '女性成长', '都市修仙', '悬疑', '科幻', '脑洞', '犯罪', '灾难', '青春', '武侠', '宫斗', '亲情']],
  ['style', '风格', ['智能推荐', '真人', '漫剧']],
  ['aud', '核心面向人群', ['智能推荐', '男频', '女频']]
];
/** 剧集规模：集数为档位，其余按比例联动，各有独立上下限 */
const EP_STEPS = [10, 20, 30];
const MIN_PER_EP = 2;                     // 每集约 2 分钟，用于估总时长
function scaleDefaults(ep) {
  return {
    ep,
    chap: Math.max(1, Math.round(ep / 5)),        // 默认 5 集 1 章
    branch: 30,                                   // 支线占比 %
    ending: Math.max(1, Math.round(ep * 0.10)),   // 默认 10%
    fork: Math.max(1, Math.round(ep * 0.20))      // 默认 20%
  };
}
/** 各项上下限（随集数变化） */
function scaleLimits(ep) {
  return {
    chap: [1, ep],                                 // 最大 = 集数 100%
    branch: [0, 60],
    ending: [1, Math.max(1, Math.round(ep * 0.20))],   // 最大 20%
    fork: [1, Math.max(1, Math.round(ep * 0.50))]      // 最大 50%
  };
}
/** 创作页表单态（全部可不填，走默认逻辑） */
const CFG = {
  file: null,
  fileText: '',   // 参考剧本正文（上传后异步读入，生成时随 prompt 一起喂给模型）
  preset: { setting: '智能推荐', bg: '智能推荐', theme: '智能推荐', style: '智能推荐', aud: '智能推荐' },
  dir: '竖屏',
  scale: scaleDefaults(10),
  pop: ''    // '' | 'preset' | 'scale'
};
const presetPicked = () => PRESET_GROUPS.filter(([k]) => CFG.preset[k] !== '智能推荐').length;
/** chip 文案：始终带字段名，默认态显示「风格：智能推荐」，选过则显示已选项 */
const presetLabel = () => {
  const picked = PRESET_GROUPS.filter(([k]) => CFG.preset[k] !== '智能推荐');
  if (!picked.length) return '风格：智能推荐';
  const [, , ] = picked[0];
  const txt = picked.slice(0, 2).map(([k]) => CFG.preset[k]).join(' · ');
  return '风格：' + txt + (picked.length > 2 ? ` +${picked.length - 2}` : '');
};

/* ---------- Codex 风格滑针（展示具体数量 + 档位圆点） ---------- */
function slider(key, label, val, min, max, unit, steps) {
  const pct = max === min ? 100 : (val - min) / (max - min) * 100;
  const dots = (steps || [0.25, 0.5, 0.75]).map(p =>
    `<i class="sd" style="left:${p * 100}%"></i>`).join('');
  return `<div class="sl" data-sl="${key}" data-min="${min}" data-max="${max}" data-unit="${unit}">
    <div class="sl-hd"><span class="sl-lb">${label}</span>
      <b class="sl-val">${val}<em>${unit}</em></b>
      <button class="sl-rs" data-slrs="${key}" title="恢复默认">↺</button></div>
    <div class="sl-track"><i class="sl-fill" style="width:${pct}%"></i>${dots}
      <i class="sl-thumb" style="left:${pct}%"></i></div>
    <div class="sl-rng"><span>${min}${unit}</span><span>${max}${unit}</span></div>
  </div>`;
}

/** Yoroll 式：分组标题独占一行 + 等宽网格卡片（icon 在上、名字在下） */
const GRP_ICON = { setting: '🎬', bg: '🏙', theme: '✨', style: '🎨', aud: '👥' };
const OPT_ICON = {
  '智能推荐': '✦', '逆袭': '🚀', '复仇': '🗡', '打脸虐渣': '💥', '解密': '🔎', '豪门恩怨': '💎',
  '追妻': '💐', '权谋': '♟', '寻亲': '🧭', '家庭伦理': '🏠', '先婚后爱': '💍', '暗恋成真': '💌', '虐恋': '💔',
  '都市': '🌆', '家庭': '🍚', '豪门': '🏛', '宫廷': '🏯', '职场': '💼', '农村': '🌾', '江湖': '⚔',
  '仙魔': '🔮', '校园': '🎒', '战场': '🎖', '末世': '☢',
  '玄幻仙侠': '🐉', '女性成长': '🌷', '都市修仙': '🌃', '悬疑': '🕯', '科幻': '🛸', '脑洞': '🌀',
  '犯罪': '🚨', '灾难': '🌊', '青春': '🍧', '武侠': '🥋', '宫斗': '👑', '亲情': '🤝',
  '真人': '🎥', '漫剧': '🖌', '男频': '🧔', '女频': '👩'
};
/** 选项渲染：纯文字标签（plain）/ 带 icon 标签，一律流式排布 */
function grid(k, opts, plain) {
  return `<div class="ogs ${plain ? 'plain' : ''}">${opts.map(o => `
    <button class="os ${CFG.preset[k] === o ? 'on' : ''} ${o === '智能推荐' ? 'auto' : ''}" data-pk="${k}" data-pv="${esc(o)}">
      ${plain ? '' : `<i>${OPT_ICON[o] || '·'}</i>`}${esc(o)}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;
}
/** 少量互斥项走分段器（风格 / 人群 / 横竖屏） */
function segOf(k, opts) {
  return `<div class="seg wide">${opts.map(o =>
    `<button class="${CFG.preset[k] === o ? 'on' : ''}" data-pk="${k}" data-pv="${esc(o)}">${esc(o)}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;
}

function popPreset() {
  const TAG_GROUPS = PRESET_GROUPS.filter(([k]) => k === 'setting' || k === 'bg' || k === 'theme');
  const styleOpts = (PRESET_GROUPS.find(([k]) => k === 'style') || [])[2] || [];
  const audOpts = (PRESET_GROUPS.find(([k]) => k === 'aud') || [])[2] || [];
  return `<div class="pop-panel" id="popPreset">
    <div class="pp-body">
      <div class="prow2">
        <div class="pgrp">
          <div class="pgt">🎨 风格<em>画面呈现形态</em></div>
          ${segOf('style', styleOpts)}
        </div>
        <div class="pgrp">
          <div class="pgt">📱 游戏方向<em>暂仅支持竖屏</em></div>
          <div class="seg wide">
            <button class="on">竖屏</button>
            <button class="dis" data-mock="横屏后续拓展 · 我是假的 等研发爸爸们做">横屏</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="pgrp">
        <div class="pgt">👥 核心面向人群<em>未选即「智能推荐」</em></div>
        ${segOf('aud', audOpts)}
      </div>
      ${TAG_GROUPS.map(([k, name, opts]) => `
        <div class="pgrp">
          <div class="pgt">${GRP_ICON[k]} ${name}<em>未选即「智能推荐」，走默认逻辑</em></div>
          ${grid(k, opts, true)}
        </div>`).join('')}
    </div>
    <div class="pp-ft"><span class="ft-tip">全部可不填</span><button class="btn sm pri" data-ppclose>完成</button></div>
  </div>`;
}

function popScale() {
  const s = CFG.scale, L = scaleLimits(s.ep);
  const mins = s.ep * MIN_PER_EP;
  return `<div class="pop-panel" id="popScale">
    <div class="pp-body">
      <div class="pgrp">
        <div class="pgt">🎞 剧本集数<em>选定后，下方章节 / 支线 / 结局 / 分叉自动联动</em></div>
        <div class="seg big">${EP_STEPS.map(e =>
    `<button class="${s.ep === e ? 'on' : ''}" data-ep="${e}">${e} 集<em>约 ${e * MIN_PER_EP} 分钟</em></button>`).join('')}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="sum">
        <span class="si"><b>${mins}</b>分钟</span>
        <span class="si"><b>${s.chap}</b>章</span>
        <span class="si"><b>${s.branch}%</b>支线</span>
        <span class="si"><b>${s.ending}</b>结局</span>
        <span class="si"><b>${s.fork}</b>分叉</span>
      </div>
      <div class="pgrp">
        <div class="pgt">🎚 精细调节<em>各项有独立上限，可单独微调</em></div>
        <div class="slgrid">
          ${slider('chap', '章节数量', s.chap, L.chap[0], L.chap[1], ' 章')}
          ${slider('branch', '支线占比', s.branch, L.branch[0], L.branch[1], '%')}
          ${slider('ending', '结局数量', s.ending, L.ending[0], L.ending[1], ' 个')}
          ${slider('fork', '分叉节点', s.fork, L.fork[0], L.fork[1], ' 个')}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="pp-ft"><button class="btn sm" data-screset>↺ 恢复默认</button><button class="btn sm pri" data-ppclose>完成</button></div>
  </div>`;
}

            🔢 规模：${s.ep} 集 · ${s.chap} 章 · ${s.ending} 结局 <i class="ca">▾</i></span>
        </div>
        <button class="send" id="btnGen">开始创作 <span>→</span></button>
      </div>
      <div class="pop-wrap ${CFG.pop ? 'pw-' + CFG.pop : ''}" id="popWrap">${CFG.pop === 'preset' ? popPreset() : CFG.pop === 'scale' ? popScale() : ''}</div>
    </div>

    <div class="ideas">
      <span class="ilb">灵感速吸</span>
      <div class="itrack">${IDEAS.map(([e, t]) => `<span class="chip" data-idea="${esc(t)}"><span class="ce">${e}</span>${esc(t)}</span>`).join('')}</div>
    </div>

      <div class="de-thumb"><img src="${PROJECT.cover}"></div>
      <div class="de-txt">

        <p>${esc(PROJECT.subtitle)} · ${st.total} 节点 / ${st.endings} 结局 / ${st.chapters} 章 · 真机预览已接通</p>
      </div>
      <div class="de-acts">
        <button class="de-play" id="demoPlay">▶ 预览</button>
        <button class="de-go" id="demoEdit">进入编辑器 →</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>`;
  bindCreate();

}

/* ---- 首页标题入场：AI 从天而降砸开「创作你的 / 互动影游」 ----
 * 先量出 AI 的自然宽度写进 --ai-w，再上 mg-go 触发动画；每次进入首页都播 */
let _megaT = [];
function playMegaIntro() {
  const h = $('#megaTitle'); if (!h) return;
  _megaT.forEach(clearTimeout); _megaT = [];
  const ai = h.querySelector('.mg-ai');
  // 量宽：临时放开约束，读到自然宽度后写回 CSS 变量
  ai.style.cssText = 'width:auto;opacity:1;position:absolute;visibility:hidden';
  const w = ai.getBoundingClientRect().width;
  ai.style.cssText = '';
  h.style.setProperty('--ai-w', (w || 60) + 'px');
  h.className = 'mega mg-anim';
  void h.offsetWidth;                       // 强制重排，确保初始态生效
  _megaT.push(setTimeout(() => { h.classList.add('mg-go', 'mg-shake'); }, 30));
  _megaT.push(setTimeout(() => { h.className = 'mega mg-done'; }, 2720));
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------
 * 视图更新分级（铁律：浮层开着时，绝不重建 #stage）
 *   L1 只改数值      → 改文本节点 / style      → syncScale()
 *   L2 只改选中态    → classList.toggle        → 就地 toggle
 *   L3 改上下限      → 改 dataset + 刻度文案   → applyEp()
 *   L4 开/关浮层     → 只重写 #popWrap 一个容器 → renderPop()
 *   L5 整页重建      → 仅初次进入 create 页时
 * ------------------------------------------------------------------- */

/** L4：只重写浮层容器 + 同步 chip 态，textarea / 图片 / 灵感区一概不动 */
function renderPop() {
  const wrap = $('#popWrap');
  if (!wrap) return;
  wrap.className = 'pop-wrap ' + (CFG.pop ? 'pw-' + CFG.pop : '');
  wrap.innerHTML = CFG.pop === 'preset' ? popPreset() : CFG.pop === 'scale' ? popScale() : '';
  wrap.onclick = e => e.stopPropagation();
  syncChips();
  bindPreset(); bindScale();
  if (CFG.pop) { document.removeEventListener('click', closePopOnce); document.addEventListener('click', closePopOnce, { once: true }); }
}

/** 上传 chip 就地同步（文件名 / 清除按钮），不重建整页 */
function syncUpChip() {
  const chip = $('#upChip'); if (!chip) return;
  chip.classList.toggle('has', !!CFG.file);
  const ft = chip.querySelector('.ft');
  if (ft) ft.textContent = `📎 ${CFG.file || '上传参考小说 / 剧本'}`;
  const x = chip.querySelector('.x');
  if (x) {
    x.classList.toggle('hide', !CFG.file);
    x.onclick = e => {
      e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
      CFG.file = null; CFG.fileText = '';
      const inp = $('#upIn'); if (inp) inp.value = '';
      syncUpChip();
    };
  }
}

/** 同步两个 chip 的文案 + has/open 态（纯文本节点写入，无 innerHTML） */
function syncChips() {
  const s = CFG.scale;
  const cp = $('[data-pop="preset"]');
  if (cp) {
    cp.firstChild.nodeValue = `🎨 ${presetLabel()} `;
    cp.classList.toggle('has', !!presetPicked());
    cp.classList.toggle('open', CFG.pop === 'preset');
  }
  const cs = $('[data-pop="scale"]');
  if (cs) {
    cs.firstChild.nodeValue = `🔢 规模：${s.ep} 集 · ${s.chap} 章 · ${s.ending} 结局 `;
    cs.classList.toggle('has', s.ep !== 10);
    cs.classList.toggle('open', CFG.pop === 'scale');
  }
}

/** L3：切集数档位 —— 上下限变了，但仍不重建 DOM，只改 dataset + 刻度文案 */
function applyEp(ep) {
  CFG.scale = scaleDefaults(ep);
  const p = $('#popScale'); if (!p) return;
  p.querySelectorAll('[data-ep]').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('on', +b.dataset.ep === ep));
  const L = scaleLimits(ep);
  p.querySelectorAll('[data-sl]').forEach(sl => {
    const k = sl.dataset.sl, unit = sl.dataset.unit || '';
    sl.dataset.min = L[k][0]; sl.dataset.max = L[k][1];
    const rng = sl.querySelectorAll('.sl-rng span');
    if (rng.length === 2) { rng[0].textContent = L[k][0] + unit; rng[1].textContent = L[k][1] + unit; }
  });
  syncScale();
}

/** 整页重建（L5）—— 仅初次进入 create 页用；任何浮层开着时禁止调用 */
function repaintCreate() {
  const keep = $('#pIn') ? $('#pIn').value : '';
  renderCreate();
  if (keep && $('#pIn')) $('#pIn').value = keep;
}

function bindCreate() {
  $('#stage').querySelectorAll('[data-idea]').forEach(c => c.onclick = () => {
    $('#pIn').value = c.dataset.idea + '，多结局互动影游，带养成数值与广告解锁支线。';
    $('#pIn').focus();
  });
  const openEdit = ev => { if (ev) ev.stopPropagation(); state.view = 'editor'; state.step = 'story'; renderShell(); };
  const openPlay = ev => { if (ev) ev.stopPropagation(); state.view = 'play'; renderShell(); };
  $('#demoEntry').onclick = openEdit;
  $('#demoEdit').onclick = openEdit;
  $('#demoPlay').onclick = openPlay;

  /* 上传：真选文件，读出正文供生成时作为参考剧本喂给模型 */
  const upIn = $('#upIn');
  if (upIn) upIn.onchange = e => {
    const f = e.target.files && e.target.files[0];
    if (!f) return;
    if (f.size > 10 * 1024 * 1024) { toast('文件超过 10MB 上限'); upIn.value = ''; return; }
    CFG.file = f.name; CFG.fileText = ''; syncUpChip();
    const fr = new FileReader();
    fr.onload = () => {
      CFG.fileText = String(fr.result || '').trim();
      toast(`已读取参考剧本 · ${CFG.file}（${(CFG.fileText.length / 1000).toFixed(1)}k 字）`);
    };
    fr.onerror = () => { CFG.fileText = ''; toast('参考文件读取失败，本次生成将不带参考剧本'); };
    fr.readAsText(f, 'utf-8');
  };
  syncUpChip();

  /* chip → 浮层开合（L4：只换 #popWrap，绝不重建 #stage） */
  $('#stage').querySelectorAll('[data-pop]').forEach(c => c.onclick = e => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    CFG.pop = CFG.pop === c.dataset.pop ? '' : c.dataset.pop;
    renderPop();
  });
  const wrap = $('#popWrap');
  if (wrap) wrap.onclick = e => e.stopPropagation();
  document.addEventListener('click', closePopOnce, { once: true });

  bindPreset(); bindScale();
  const bg = $('#btnGen');
  if (bg) bg.onclick = () => startGen();
}

/* =======================================================================
 * 真实全链路生成：POST /api/gen（SSE）→ 剧本 / 元素 / 分镜
 * 服务端调用万擎推理平台，key 不落前端
 * ======================================================================= */
const GEN = { running: false, out: null, steps: null, t0: 0 };

/**
 * 生成服务地址。静态托管（frontend-cloud）上没有 Node 后端，
 * 需把 AI 生成打向一台可达的 Node 服务：
 *   ①  ?api=http://host:8899  （落地到 localStorage，一次即可）
 *   ②  window.GEN_API_BASE    （部署时写进 index.html）
 *   ③  默认同源（本地 node server.js 的情况）
 */
function apiBase() {
  try {
    const q = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('api');
    if (q !== null) { q ? localStorage.setItem('jgy_api_base', q) : localStorage.removeItem('jgy_api_base'); }
    return localStorage.getItem('jgy_api_base') || window.GEN_API_BASE || '';
  } catch (_) { return window.GEN_API_BASE || ''; }
}

/* =======================================================================
 * 草稿仓储：真实生成的剧本落成「历史项目」里的草稿，可继续编辑
 * ======================================================================= */
const DRAFTS = { list: [], cur: null };
const DRAFT_KEY = 'jgy_drafts_v1';
try { DRAFTS.list = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(DRAFT_KEY) || '[]'); } catch (e) { DRAFTS.list = []; }
// 上次页面被关掉时留下的「生成中」卡片：本轮不可能还在跑，标记为中断
DRAFTS.list.forEach(d => { if (d.status === 'generating') { d.status = 'failed'; d.err = '生成过程被中断（页面已关闭），请重新生成'; } });
function saveDrafts() { try { localStorage.setItem(DRAFT_KEY, JSON.stringify(DRAFTS.list)); } catch (e) { } }
function nowStr() {
  const d = new Date(), z = n => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${z(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${z(d.getDate())} ${z(d.getHours())}:${z(d.getMinutes())}`;
}
/** 开始生成即建草稿卡（状态 generating），生成完再回填 */
function draftCreate(prompt, panel) {
  const d = {
    id: 'dft' + Date.now(), status: 'generating',
    title: '生成中…', sub: '', prompt, panel,
    created: nowStr(), updated: nowStr(),
    story: null, check: null
  };
  DRAFTS.list.unshift(d); DRAFTS.cur = d; saveDrafts();
  return d;
}
function draftFinish(d, story, check) {
  d.status = 'draft'; d.story = story; d.check = check;
  d.title = story.title || '未命名'; d.sub = story.sub || '';
  d.updated = nowStr(); saveDrafts();
}
function draftFail(d, msg) { d.status = 'failed'; d.err = msg; d.updated = nowStr(); saveDrafts(); }
function draftById(id) { return DRAFTS.list.find(x => x.id === id); }

/* ---------- 等待期彩蛋：游戏行业 / 英雄背景冷知识 200 条 ----------
   数据取自内网 aiminigame-onepage 的 game-trivia-100 + hero-trivia-100 */
const TRIVIA = { list: [], i: 0, timer: 0, seen: [] };
fetch('data/trivia.json').then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
  TRIVIA.list = d;
  // 洗牌，保证一次会话里不重复
  for (let k = d.length - 1; k > 0; k--) { const j = Math.random() * (k + 1) | 0;[d[k], d[j]] = [d[j], d[k]]; }
  if (GEN.running) paintTrivia(1);
}).catch(() => { });

function triviaHTML() {
  const t = TRIVIA.list[TRIVIA.i % (TRIVIA.list.length || 1)];
  if (!t) return '';
  return `<div class="tv" id="tvBox">
    <div class="tv-hd"><span class="tv-lb">🎲 等待中 · 来点冷知识</span>
      <span class="tv-tag">${esc(t.from)}</span><span class="tv-tag t2">${esc(t.tag)}</span>
      <span class="tv-no">${(TRIVIA.i % TRIVIA.list.length) + 1} / ${TRIVIA.list.length}</span></div>
    <h4 class="tv-t">${esc(t.title)}</h4>
    <p class="tv-c">${esc(t.content)}</p>
    <div class="tv-ft"><span class="tv-bar"><i></i></span>
      <button class="tv-next" id="tvNext">🎲 再看一个</button></div>
  </div>`;
}
/* 冷知识节点常驻在 document 上，不随 #stage 每秒重绘而重建 —— 否则会闪 */
let TV_NODE = null, TV_SWAP = 0;
function tvBind(node) {
  const b = node.querySelector('.tv-next');
  if (b) b.onclick = () => { tvFlip(); restartTrivia(); };
  // 换内容后重启 9 秒进度条动画（innerHTML 替换会保留旧动画进度）
  const bar = node.querySelector('.tv-bar i');
  if (bar) { bar.style.animation = 'none'; void bar.offsetWidth; bar.style.animation = ''; }
}
/** 首次挂载 / 搬运（不触发动画）；翻页走 tvFlip 的渐隐渐出 */
function paintTrivia(force) {
  const wrap = $('#tvWrap');
  if (!wrap || !TRIVIA.list.length) return;
  // 内容没换 → 把已存在的节点原样搬回新的 wrap，不动 innerHTML（零闪烁）
  if (TV_NODE && !force) {
    if (TV_NODE.parentNode !== wrap) wrap.appendChild(TV_NODE);
    return;
  }
  const holder = document.createElement('div');
  holder.innerHTML = triviaHTML();
  TV_NODE = holder.firstElementChild;
  wrap.innerHTML = '';
  wrap.appendChild(TV_NODE);
  tvBind(TV_NODE);
}
/** 翻页：老卡淡出 → 换文案 → 新卡淡入，全程同一个 DOM 节点，不会闪 */
function tvFlip() {
  if (!TV_NODE || !TRIVIA.list.length) return;
  const my = ++TV_SWAP, n = TV_NODE;
  // 直接写 inline opacity：不依赖 class 特异性与样式表加载时序，最稳
  n.style.transition = 'opacity .26s ease';
  n.style.opacity = '0';
  setTimeout(() => {
    if (my !== TV_SWAP || !TV_NODE) return;
    TRIVIA.i++;
    const holder = document.createElement('div');
    holder.innerHTML = triviaHTML();
    // 只换内容，节点本身保留 → 高度与位置稳定，只有透明度在动
    n.innerHTML = holder.firstElementChild.innerHTML;
    tvBind(n);
    n.style.opacity = '1';
  }, 270);
}
function restartTrivia() {
  clearInterval(TRIVIA.timer);
  TRIVIA.timer = setInterval(tvFlip, 9000);
}
function stopTrivia() { clearInterval(TRIVIA.timer); TRIVIA.timer = 0; TV_NODE = null; }
/** 局部刷新某一步骤行（状态点 / 文案 / 字数），成功返回 true */
function patchStep(k) {
  const row = $('#stage') && $('#stage').querySelector(`.grow[data-k="${k}"]`);
  if (!row) return false;
  const s = GEN.steps[k] || {};
  if (row.className !== `grow ${s.st}`) row.className = `grow ${s.st}`;
  const ic = row.querySelector('.gic');
  const want = s.st === 'done' ? '✓' : s.st === 'run' ? '<i class="spin"></i>' : '○';
  if (ic && ic.innerHTML !== want) ic.innerHTML = want;
  const em = row.querySelector('.gtx em');
  if (em && s.msg && em.textContent !== s.msg) em.textContent = s.msg;
  let cnt = row.querySelector('.gcnt');
  if (s.st === 'run') {
    if (!cnt) { cnt = document.createElement('span'); cnt.className = 'gcnt'; row.appendChild(cnt); }
    const txt = `思考 ${s.think || 0} 字 · 正文 ${s.text || 0} 字`;
    if (cnt.textContent !== txt) cnt.textContent = txt;
  } else if (cnt) cnt.remove();
  return true;
}
function tickSecs() {
  const n = $('#genSecs');
  if (n) n.textContent = ((Date.now() - GEN.t0) / 1000).toFixed(0);
}
const GEN_STEPS = [
  ['story', '① 剧本', '推导档位 · 生成七件骨架 / 9 节拍 / 结局矩阵'],
  ['element', '② 元素拆解', '角色 · 场景 · 道具 三列结构卡 + 结构化生图提示词'],
  ['shot', '③ 分镜节点', '编排节点图 · choice 分叉 · 图谱完整性自检']
];

async function startGen() {
  const raw = ($('#pIn') && $('#pIn').value || '').trim();
  if (raw.length < 20) { toast('再多写一点，至少 20 字 AI 才拆得准'); $('#pIn') && $('#pIn').focus(); return; }
  if (CFG.file && !CFG.fileText) { toast('参考剧本还在读取中，稍等一秒再点'); return; }
  if (GEN.running) return;

  const s = CFG.scale, p = CFG.preset || {};
  // 面板五项标签：「智能推荐」= 用户没选，不写死给模型，交由 AI 自行推导
  const pick = k => (p[k] && p[k] !== '智能推荐') ? p[k] : '';
  const panel = {};
  PRESET_GROUPS.forEach(([k, label]) => { const v = pick(k); if (v) panel[label] = v; });
  panel['画面比例'] = CFG.dir === '横屏' ? '16:9' : '9:16';
  panel['剧集规模'] = `${s.ep} 集 / ${s.chap} 章 / ${s.ending} 结局 / ${s.fork} 分叉 / 支线 ${s.branch}%`;

  // 参考剧本：截断到 40k 字，避免顶爆上下文
  const REF_MAX = 40000;
  const refRaw = (CFG.fileText || '').trim();
  const ref = refRaw.length > REF_MAX ? refRaw.slice(0, REF_MAX) + '\n…（参考文本过长，已截断）' : refRaw;
  if (refRaw) panel['参考剧本'] = `${CFG.file}（${refRaw.length} 字${refRaw.length > REF_MAX ? '，已截断至 4 万字' : ''}）`;

  let prompt = `${raw}\n\n要求：共 ${s.ep} 集，约 ${s.chap} 章，${s.ending} 个结局，${s.fork} 个分叉节点，支线占比约 ${s.branch}%。`;
  if (ref) prompt += `\n\n【参考剧本 · ${CFG.file}】\n以下是用户提供的参考素材，请以它为故事底本进行改编（沿用其人物、世界观与主线走向），而不是另起炉灶：\n"""\n${ref}\n"""`;

  GEN.running = true; GEN.t0 = Date.now(); GEN.full = false;
  GEN.draft = draftCreate(raw, panel);   // 开始生成就落一张草稿卡（只存用户原文，不含参考剧本全文）
  GEN.abort = new AbortController();
  GEN.cancelled = false; GEN.done = 0; GEN.err = 0;
  GEN.out = { story: null, element: null, shot: null, audit: null };
  GEN.steps = { story: { st: 'wait' }, element: { st: 'wait' }, shot: { st: 'wait' } };
  renderGenProgress();
  TRIVIA.i = Math.random() * (TRIVIA.list.length || 1) | 0;
  TV_NODE = null; paintTrivia(1); restartTrivia();

  try {
    const resp = await fetch(apiBase() + '/api/gen', {
      method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ prompt, panel, full: GEN.full }),
      signal: GEN.abort.signal
    });
    if (!resp.ok || !resp.body) throw new Error('服务未响应（' + resp.status + '）');
    const rd = resp.body.getReader(), dec = new TextDecoder();
    let buf = '';
    for (; ;) {
      const { value, done } = await rd.read();
      if (done) break;
      buf += dec.decode(value, { stream: true });
      let ix;
      while ((ix = buf.indexOf('\n\n')) >= 0) {
        const blk = buf.slice(0, ix); buf = buf.slice(ix + 2);
        const ev = (blk.match(/^event: (\w+)/m) || [])[1];
        const dl = (blk.match(/^data: (.*)$/m) || [])[1];
        if (!ev || !dl) continue;
        let d; try { d = JSON.parse(dl); } catch (e) { continue; }
        onGenEvent(ev, d);
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {
    if (GEN.cancelled || (e && e.name === 'AbortError')) {
      // 用户主动取消：任务废弃，草稿卡一并丢掉，退回首页
      GEN.running = false; stopTrivia();
      if (GEN.draft) { DRAFTS.list = DRAFTS.list.filter(x => x.id !== GEN.draft.id); saveDrafts(); GEN.draft = null; }
      state.view = 'create'; renderShell(); toast('已取消生成，任务已废弃');
      return;
    }
    GEN.err = String(e.message || e);
    if (GEN.draft) draftFail(GEN.draft, GEN.err);
    stopTrivia();
    renderGenProgress();
  }
  GEN.running = false;
}

function onGenEvent(ev, d) {
  if (ev === 'stage') {
    const prev = GEN.steps[d.step] || {};
    GEN.steps[d.step] = { st: d.status === 'done' ? 'done' : 'run', msg: d.msg, think: prev.think, text: prev.text };
    if (patchStep(d.step)) return;          // 局部更新成功就不整页重绘
  }
  else if (ev === 'tick') {
    const s = GEN.steps[d.step] || (GEN.steps[d.step] = { st: 'run' });
    s.think = d.think; s.text = d.text;
    // 只改数字，绝不重绘整页 —— 否则冷知识会每秒闪一次
    patchStep(d.step); tickSecs();
    return;
  }
  else if (ev === 'story' || ev === 'element' || ev === 'shot') {
    GEN.out[ev] = d.data;
    if (ev === 'shot') GEN.out.audit = d.audit;
    if (ev === 'story') GEN.out.check = d.check;
  }
  else if (ev === 'all') {
    GEN.done = true;
    if (GEN.draft && GEN.out.story) {
      draftFinish(GEN.draft, GEN.out.story, GEN.out.check);
      // 剧本一完成就直接进编辑器故事页（草稿态），不停在结果卡上
      stopTrivia();
      openDraft(GEN.draft.id);
      return;
    }
  }
  else if (ev === 'err') { GEN.err = d.msg; if (GEN.draft) draftFail(GEN.draft, d.msg); }
  renderGenProgress();
}

/** 取消生成：中断请求 → 后端掐断模型调用 → 废弃草稿 */
function cancelGen() {
  if (!GEN.running || GEN.cancelled) return;
  GEN.cancelled = true;
  try { GEN.abort && GEN.abort.abort(); } catch (e) { }
  stopTrivia();
  // 立即给出反馈，不等 fetch 的 reject
  const h = $('.pane.gen h1'); if (h) h.textContent = '正在取消…';
}

/** 打开某个草稿：进入编辑器故事页，按真实生成的剧本渲染 */
function openDraft(id) {
  const d = draftById(id);
  if (!d || !d.story) { toast('该草稿还没有剧本内容'); return; }
  DRAFTS.cur = d;
  state.view = 'editor'; state.step = 'story'; state.src = 'draft';
  state.docOpen = true; state.inputOpen = false;   // 剧本正文默认展开，输入参数折叠
  GEN.done = 0; GEN.err = 0;
  renderShell();
}
function renderGenProgress() {
  const secs = ((Date.now() - GEN.t0) / 1000).toFixed(0);
  const rows = (GEN.full ? GEN_STEPS : GEN_STEPS.slice(0, 1)).map(([k, t, sub]) => {
    const s = GEN.steps[k] || { st: 'wait' };
    const ic = s.st === 'done' ? '✓' : s.st === 'run' ? '<i class="spin"></i>' : '○';
    const cnt = s.st === 'run'
      ? `<span class="gcnt">思考 ${s.think || 0} 字 · 正文 ${s.text || 0} 字</span>` : '';
    return `<div class="genrow ${s.st}">
      <span class="gic">${ic}</span>
      <div class="gtx"><b>${t}</b><em>${esc(s.msg || sub)}</em></div>${cnt}</div>`;
  }).join('');

  const o = GEN.out || {};
  const done = GEN.done && o.story;
  let result = '';
  if (done) {
    const g = o.story.gear || {}, a = o.audit || {};
    result = `<div class="gres">
      <div class="gres-hd"><b>${esc(o.story.title || '')}</b><span>${esc(o.story.sub || '')}</span></div>
      <div class="gres-kv">
        <span>${esc(g.structure || '')} · ${g.units || 0} 单位</span>
        <span>${esc(g.ip || '')}</span><span>变现：${esc(g.monetize || '无')}</span>
        <span>${(o.story.endings || []).length} 结局</span>
        <span>${(o.story.beats || []).length} 节拍</span>
        <span>${(o.story.roles || []).length} 角色</span>
        ${GEN.full ? `<span>${(o.element && o.element.chars || []).length} 角色卡 / ${(o.element && o.element.scenes || []).length} 场景 / ${(o.element && o.element.props || []).length} 道具</span>
        <span>${(o.shot && o.shot.nodes || []).length} 分镜节点</span>
        <span class="${a.ok ? 'ok' : 'bad'}">图谱自检 ${a.ok ? '通过' : '孤儿' + (a.orphans || []).length + ' / 死胡同' + (a.deadEnds || []).length}</span>` : ''}
      </div>
      <div class="gres-q">「${esc((o.story.conflict || {}).theme || '')}」</div>
      ${(() => {
        const ck = o.check || {};
        if (ck.ok) return `<div class="gchk ok">✓ 模板 v3 恒定件校验全部通过（12 项）</div>`;
        if (!ck.bad) return '';
        return `<div class="gchk bad"><b>⚠ 恒定件未通过 ${ck.bad.length} 项</b>${ck.bad.map(x => `<span>${esc(x)}</span>`).join('')}</div>`;
      })()}
      <div class="gres-ft">
        <button class="btn" id="genBack">← 改一版</button>
        <button class="btn pri shine" id="genOpen">进入编辑器 →</button>
      </div></div>`;
  } else if (GEN.err) {
    result = `<div class="gerr">✕ ${esc(GEN.err)}<div class="gres-ft"><button class="btn" id="genBack">← 返回重试</button></div></div>`;
  }

  $('#stage').innerHTML = `<div class="pane gen">
    <span class="eyebrow"><span class="dot"></span> ${done ? '真实模型已生成' : '真实模型生成中'} · glm-5.3</span>
    <h1 class="mega">${done ? '生成完成 🎬' : GEN.err ? '生成中断' : '正在为你拆解剧本…'}</h1>
    <p class="sub">按《互动影游剧本模板 v3》${GEN.full ? '全链路' : '生成剧本'}，全程真实调用内部大模型 · 已用 <b class="secs" id="genSecs">${secs}</b>s</p>
    <div class="glist">${rows}</div>
    ${done || GEN.err ? '' : `<div id="tvWrap"></div>
    <div class="gcancel"><button class="cxbtn" id="btnCancel" title="终止本次生成并废弃任务">✕</button>
      <span>终止生成</span></div>`}
    ${result}
  </div>`;
  if (done || GEN.err) stopTrivia(); else paintTrivia();
  const bc = $('#btnCancel');
  if (bc) bc.onclick = () => cancelGen();
  const gb = $('#genBack'); if (gb) gb.onclick = () => { GEN.done = 0; GEN.err = 0; renderCreate(); };
  const go = $('#genOpen'); if (go) go.onclick = () => { toast('真实数据已生成，接入编辑器渲染下一步做'); };
}
function closePopOnce() { if (CFG.pop) { CFG.pop = ''; if (state.view === 'create') renderPop(); } }

function bindPreset() {
  /* L2：选标签只切同组选中态 + 同步 chip，零 DOM 重建 */
  $('#stage').querySelectorAll('[data-pk]').forEach(t => t.onclick = ev => {
    ev.stopPropagation();
    const k = t.dataset.pk;
    CFG.preset[k] = t.dataset.pv;
    $('#stage').querySelectorAll(`[data-pk="${k}"]`).forEach(o =>
      o.classList.toggle('on', o.dataset.pv === CFG.preset[k]));
    syncChips();
  });
  $('#stage').querySelectorAll('[data-ppclose]').forEach(b => b.onclick = ev => {
    ev.stopPropagation(); CFG.pop = ''; renderPop();
  });
}

function bindScale() {
  const p = $('#popScale'); if (!p) return;
  /* L3：集数档位 → 联动上下限，但只改 dataset + 文案，不重建 DOM */
  p.querySelectorAll('[data-ep]').forEach(t => t.onclick = ev => {
    ev.stopPropagation(); applyEp(+t.dataset.ep);
  });
  p.querySelector('[data-screset]').onclick = ev => { ev.stopPropagation(); applyEp(CFG.scale.ep); };
  p.querySelectorAll('[data-slrs]').forEach(b => b.onclick = ev => {
    ev.stopPropagation();
    const k = b.dataset.slrs; CFG.scale[k] = scaleDefaults(CFG.scale.ep)[k];
    syncScale();   // 单项复位不改上下限 → 局部同步即可，不重绘
  });

  /* 拖拽滑针：min/max 每次实时读 dataset（applyEp 会改），避免闭包读到旧上下限 */
  p.querySelectorAll('[data-sl]').forEach(sl => {
    const key = sl.dataset.sl;
    const track = sl.querySelector('.sl-track');
    const setBy = clientX => {
      const min = +sl.dataset.min, max = +sl.dataset.max;
      const r = track.getBoundingClientRect();
      const pct = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (clientX - r.left) / r.width));
      const v = Math.round(min + pct * (max - min));
      if (v === CFG.scale[key]) return;
      CFG.scale[key] = v;
      syncScale();
    };
    const down = ev => {
      ev.preventDefault(); ev.stopPropagation();
      document.body.classList.add('dragging');
      setBy(ev.clientX);
      const mv = e2 => setBy(e2.clientX);
      const up = () => {
        document.removeEventListener('mousemove', mv);
        document.removeEventListener('mouseup', up);
        document.body.classList.remove('dragging');
        // 松手不再整页重绘（那正是闪烁的来源），只补一次同步
        syncScale();
      };
      document.addEventListener('mousemove', mv);
      document.addEventListener('mouseup', up);
    };
    track.addEventListener('mousedown', down);
  });
}

/** 局部同步：滑针视觉 + 汇总条 + 外层 chip 文案，全程零 innerHTML 重建 */
function syncScale() {
  const s = CFG.scale, L = scaleLimits(s.ep), p = $('#popScale');
  if (p) {
    p.querySelectorAll('[data-sl]').forEach(sl => {
      const k = sl.dataset.sl, min = L[k][0], max = L[k][1];
      const pct = max === min ? 100 : (s[k] - min) / (max - min) * 100;
      sl.querySelector('.sl-fill').style.width = pct + '%';
      sl.querySelector('.sl-thumb').style.left = pct + '%';
      sl.querySelector('.sl-val').firstChild.nodeValue = s[k];
    });
    const nums = p.querySelectorAll('.sum .si b');
    if (nums.length === 5) {
      nums[0].textContent = s.ep * MIN_PER_EP;
      nums[1].textContent = s.chap;
      nums[2].textContent = s.branch + '%';
      nums[3].textContent = s.ending;
      nums[4].textContent = s.fork;
    }
  }
  const chip = $('[data-pop="scale"]');
  if (chip) chip.firstChild.nodeValue = `🔢 规模：${s.ep} 集 · ${s.chap} 章 · ${s.ending} 结局 `;
}

/* =======================================================================
 * tab2 · 历史项目
 * ======================================================================= */
function renderHistoryList() {
  const st = stats();
  const STA = {
    generating: ['生成中', 'gen'], draft: ['草稿', 'dft'],
    failed: ['生成失败', 'fail']
  };
  const draftCards = DRAFTS.list.map(d => {
    const [lb, cls] = STA[d.status] || ['草稿', 'dft'];
    const g = (d.story || {}).gear || {};
    const meta = d.status === 'draft'
      ? `${esc(g.structure || '')} · ${g.units || 0} 单位 / ${((d.story || {}).endings || []).length} 结局 / ${((d.story || {}).roles || []).length} 角色`
      : d.status === 'failed' ? esc((d.err || '').slice(0, 40)) : '正在调用大模型生成剧本…';
    return `<div class="pcard dcard ${cls}" data-draft="${d.id}">
      <div class="thumb dthumb"><span>${d.status === 'generating' ? '<i class="spin"></i>' : d.status === 'failed' ? '✕' : '📄'}</span></div>
      <h5>${esc(d.title)}<span class="dbadge ${cls}">${lb}</span></h5>
      <div class="cdesc">${esc(d.sub || '')}${d.sub ? ' · ' : ''}${meta}</div>
      <div class="foot"><span>仅剧本 · 待拆元素</span><span>${esc(d.updated)}</span></div>
      <div class="cacts">
        <button class="de-play sm" data-del="${d.id}">🗑 删除</button>
        <button class="de-go sm" data-open="${d.id}">${d.status === 'draft' ? '编辑 →' : '查看'}</button>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  }).join('');

  $('#stage').innerHTML = `<div class="pane">
    <div class="ph"><h2>历史项目</h2><span class="muted">${1 + DRAFTS.list.length} 个项目${DRAFTS.list.length ? ` · ${DRAFTS.list.length} 个草稿` : ''}</span></div>
    <div class="plist">
      ${draftCards}
      <div class="pcard" id="openProj">
        <div class="thumb"><img src="${PROJECT.cover}"></div>
        <h5>${esc(PROJECT.title)}</h5>
        <div class="cdesc">${esc(PROJECT.subtitle)} · ${st.total} 节点 / ${st.endings} 结局</div>
        <div class="foot"><span>${esc(PROJECT.stage)}</span><span>${esc(PROJECT.updated)}</span></div>
        <div class="cacts"><button class="de-play sm" id="cPlay">▶ 预览</button><button class="de-go sm" id="cEdit">编辑 →</button></div>
      </div>
      <div class="pcard new" data-go="create"><div style="text-align:center"><div style="font-size:26px">＋</div>新建互动影游</div></div>
    </div>
  </div>`;
  const goEdit = ev => { if (ev) ev.stopPropagation(); state.src = ''; state.view = 'editor'; state.step = 'story'; renderShell(); };
  $('#openProj').onclick = goEdit;
  $('#cEdit').onclick = goEdit;
  $('#cPlay').onclick = ev => { ev.stopPropagation(); state.src = ''; state.view = 'play'; renderShell(); };
  $('#stage').querySelectorAll('[data-go]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.view = b.dataset.go; renderShell(); });
  const open = id => { const d = draftById(id); if (!d) return; if (d.status !== 'draft') { toast(d.status === 'failed' ? '这份草稿生成失败了' : '还在生成中，稍等'); return; } openDraft(id); };
  $('#stage').querySelectorAll('[data-draft]').forEach(c => c.onclick = () => open(c.dataset.draft));
  $('#stage').querySelectorAll('[data-open]').forEach(b => b.onclick = ev => { ev.stopPropagation(); open(b.dataset.open); });
  $('#stage').querySelectorAll('[data-del]').forEach(b => b.onclick = ev => {
    ev.stopPropagation();
    DRAFTS.list = DRAFTS.list.filter(x => x.id !== b.dataset.del); saveDrafts();
    renderHistoryList(); toast('草稿已删除');
  });
}

/* =======================================================================
 * step1 故事 · 草稿态（真实模型产出，按模板 v3 字段逐项展示）
 * ======================================================================= */
function renderStoryDraft(d) {
  const S1 = d.story, g = S1.gear || {}, c = S1.conflict || {}, w = S1.world || {}, av = S1.av || {};
  const ck = d.check || {};
  // 与荒年记（demo 版）完全同一套排版原语：dtwrap/dtable + dlist + fold 壳
  const rows = r => `<div class="dtwrap"><table class="dtable">${r.map((x, ri) => `<tr>${x.map(cc => `<${ri === 0 ? 'th' : 'td'}>${esc(cc)}</${ri === 0 ? 'th' : 'td'}>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}</table></div>`;
  const ul = a => `<ul class="dlist">${(a || []).map(x => `<li>${esc(x)}</li>`).join('')}</ul>`;
  const kv = pairs => `<div class="kv">${pairs.filter(p => p[1]).map(p => `<dt>${esc(p[0])}</dt><dd>${esc(p[1])}</dd>`).join('')}</div>`;

  $('#stage').innerHTML = `<div class="pane">
    <div class="fold ${state.inputOpen ? 'open' : ''}" id="inFold">
    <div class="sec-head fold-head" id="inFoldHead">
      <h4><span class="caret">▸</span>输入 · 生成参数
        <span class="muted" style="font-weight:400;font-size:12px">已锁定 · 仅供查阅</span></h4>
      <div class="acts"><span class="lock-chip">🔒 不可修改</span></div>
    </div>
    <div class="fold-body">
      <div class="card" style="padding:14px">
        ${kv([['用户描述', d.prompt], ...Object.entries(d.panel || {})])}
      </div>
      <div class="callout">真实模型生成 · glm-5.3 · ${esc(d.updated)}　本稿仅含 step1 剧本，元素 / 分镜尚未生成。</div>
    </div></div>

    <div class="fold ${state.docOpen ? 'open' : ''}" id="docFold">
    <div class="sec-head fold-head" id="docFoldHead" style="margin-top:18px">
      <h4><span class="caret">▸</span>输出 · 结构化互动影游剧本（可二次编辑）
        <span class="muted" style="font-weight:400;font-size:12px">a–g 七件骨架 · ${esc(g.structure || '')} / ${(S1.units || []).length} 单位 / ${(S1.endings || []).length} 结局</span></h4>
      <div class="acts"><button class="btn sm" data-mock="重新生成剧本 · 我是假的 等研发爸爸们做">⟳ 重新生成</button><button class="btn sm" data-mock>✎ 编辑</button><button class="btn sm" data-mock>⧉</button></div>
    </div>
    <div class="fold-body">
    <div class="gres-kv" style="margin:0 0 16px">
      <span>${esc(g.structure || '')} · ${g.units || 0} 单位</span>
      <span>${esc(g.ip || '')}</span><span>变现：${esc(g.monetize || '无')}</span>
      ${g.genre ? `<span>${esc(g.genre)}</span>` : ''}
      <span>分叉 ${esc(g.forks || '')}</span><span>最低点 ${esc(g.lowPoint || '')}</span>
      <span>${(S1.endings || []).length} 结局</span>
      <span class="${ck.ok ? 'ok' : 'bad'}">恒定件 ${ck.ok ? '全通过' : (ck.bad || []).length + ' 项未过'}</span>
    </div>
    ${ck.ok ? '' : `<div class="gchk bad"><b>⚠ 模板 v3 恒定件未通过</b>${(ck.bad || []).map(x => `<span>${esc(x)}</span>`).join('')}</div>`}

    <h1>1. ${esc(S1.title || '未命名')}<div class="muted" style="font-size:14px;font-weight:400">${esc(S1.sub || '')}</div></h1>

    <h3>(a) 项目标题</h3><p>${esc(S1.title || '')}　${esc(S1.sub || '')}</p>
    <h3>(b) 项目概述</h3>${ul(S1.overview)}
    <h3>(c) 目标受众和核心卖点</h3><p class="muted" style="font-size:12px">${esc(S1.audience || '')}</p>
    ${rows([['卖点', '为什么不同 / 如何转化为交互'], ...(S1.selling || [])])}

    <h2>2. 故事</h2>
    <h3>(d1) 世界观</h3>
    ${kv([['时空', w.timespace], ['力量体系', w.power], ['碎片承载物', w.fragments]])}
    ${ul(w.rules)}
    <h3>(d2) 角色体系概览</h3>
    ${rows([['角色', '身份与叙事功能', '核心关系张力与转变', '功能位'], ...(S1.roles || [])])}
    <h3>(d3) 核心冲突</h3>
    ${kv([['外部冲突', c.outer], ['内心冲突', c.inner], ['主题命题', c.theme]])}
    <div class="callout">${esc(c.hook || '')}</div>
    <h3>(d4) 剧情走势与关键章节</h3>
    ${rows([['单位 / 章节名', '主线推进与转折', '当集分叉与实际后果'],
    ...(S1.units || []).map(u => [`第 ${u.no} 单位 · ${u.name || ''}`, u.main || '',
    (u.fork || []).map(f => `${f[0]}：${f[1] || ''} → 代价：${f[2] || ''}`).join('\n') || '—'])])}
    <h3>(d5) 核心状态（定性四档）</h3>
    ${rows([['状态', '① 最差', '②', '③', '④ 最好'],
    ...(S1.states || []).map(x => [x.name, ...(x.levels || []).slice(0, 4)])])}
    ${S1.antiShortcut ? `<div class="callout">🚫 反捷径声明：${esc(S1.antiShortcut)}</div>` : ''}
    <h3>(d6) 故事节拍（9 拍锁死）</h3>
    ${rows([['节拍', '剧情变化', '情感回报'], ...(S1.beats || []).map(b => Array.isArray(b) ? b : [b, '', ''])])}
    <h3>(d7) 主要结局类型</h3>
    ${rows([['代号', '结局名（代价名）', '类型', '触发条件'],
    ...(S1.endings || []).map(e => [e.code || '', e.name || '', e.type || '', e.cond || ''])])}

    <h2>3. 视听</h2>
    ${kv([['风格前缀', av.stylePrefix], ['色板', av.palette], ['视觉意象', av.imagery], ['镜头', av.camera], ['声音', av.sound]])}
    ${(S1.play || []).length ? `<h2>4. 玩法</h2>${ul(S1.play)}` : ''}
    ${(S1.notes || []).length ? `<h2>5. 制作备注</h2>${ul(S1.notes)}` : ''}
    </div></div>
  </div>`;
  $('#inFoldHead').onclick = ev => {
    if (ev.target.closest('[data-mock]')) return;
    state.inputOpen = !state.inputOpen;
    $('#inFold').classList.toggle('open', state.inputOpen);
  };
  $('#docFoldHead').onclick = ev => {
    if (ev.target.closest('[data-mock]')) return;
    state.docOpen = !state.docOpen;
    $('#docFold').classList.toggle('open', state.docOpen);
  };
}

/* =======================================================================

function renderStory() {
  if (state.src === 'draft' && DRAFTS.cur && DRAFTS.cur.story) return renderStoryDraft(DRAFTS.cur);
  const D = PROJECT.doc, I = PROJECT.input, st = stats();
  const rows = (r) => `<div class="dtwrap"><table class="dtable">${r.map((x, ri) => `<tr>${x.map(c => `<${ri === 0 ? 'th' : 'td'}>${esc(c)}</${ri === 0 ? 'th' : 'td'}>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}</table></div>`;
  const ul = a => `<ul class="dlist">${a.map(x => `<li>${esc(x)}</li>`).join('')}</ul>`;

  $('#stage').innerHTML = `<div class="pane">
    <div class="fold ${state.inputOpen ? 'open' : ''}" id="inFold">
    <div class="sec-head fold-head" id="inFoldHead">
      <h4><span class="caret">▸</span>输入 · 生成参数
        <span class="muted" style="font-weight:400;font-size:12px">已锁定 · 仅供查阅</span></h4>
      <div class="acts"><span class="lock-chip">🔒 不可修改</span></div>
    </div>
    <div class="fold-body">
    <div class="card" style="padding:14px">
      <div class="kv">
        <dt>参考内容</dt><dd>${esc(I.source)}</dd>
        ${['设定', '背景', '主题', '风格', '人群', '方向', '集数', '章节', '支线占比', '结局数量', '分叉节点']
      .map(k => `<dt>${k}</dt><dd><span class="tag k">${esc(I[k])}</span></dd>`).join('')}
        <dt>个性化描述</dt><dd>${esc(I.个性化描述)}</dd>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="callout">底线校验通过：无涉政 / 涉黄 / 未成年 / 真实机构与品牌命中；「妈怪」等民间说法已标注为村民口中说法并在剧情中被否定。</div>
    </div></div>

    <div class="fold ${state.docOpen ? 'open' : ''}" id="docFold">
    <div class="sec-head fold-head" id="docFoldHead" style="margin-top:18px">
      <h4><span class="caret">▸</span>输出 · 结构化互动影游剧本（可二次编辑）
        <span class="muted" style="font-weight:400;font-size:12px">a–g 七件骨架 · ${st.chapters} 章 / ${st.total} 节点</span></h4>
   <div class="acts"><button class="btn sm" data-mock>⟳ 重新生成</button><button class="btn sm" data-mock>✎ 编辑</button><button class="btn sm" data-mock>⧉</button></div>
 </div>
    <div class="fold-body">
    <h1>1. ${esc(D.a.v)}<div class="muted" style="font-size:14px;font-weight:400">${esc(D.a.sub)}</div></h1>

    <h3>(a) 项目标题</h3><p>${esc(D.a.v)}　${esc(D.a.sub)}</p>
    <h3>(b) 项目概述</h3>${ul(D.b.v)}
    <h3>(c) 目标受众和核心卖点</h3><p class="muted" style="font-size:12px">${esc(D.c.note)}</p>
    ${rows(D.c.v)}

    <h2>2. 故事</h2>
    <h3>(d1) 世界观</h3>${ul(D.d1.v)}
    <h3>(d2) 角色体系概览</h3>
    ${rows([['角色', '身份与叙事功能', '核心关系', '人物张力', '转变'], ...D.d2.rows])}
    <h3>(d3) 核心冲突</h3>${ul(D.d3.v)}
    <h3>(d4) 剧情走势与关键章节</h3>
    ${rows([['集数 / 章节名', '主线推进与转折', '当集分叉与实际后果'], ...D.d4.rows])}
    <h3>(d5) 持续支线跨集回收链</h3>${ul(D.d5.v)}
    <h3>(d6) 故事节拍（9 拍锁死）</h3>
    ${rows([['节拍', '剧情变化', '情感回报'], ...D.d6.rows])}
    <h3>(d7) 关键分支</h3>${ul(D.d7.v)}
    <h3>(d8) 主要结局类型</h3>${ul(D.d8.v)}

    <h2>3. 视听</h2>${ul(D.e.v)}
    <h2>4. 玩法</h2>${ul(D.f.v)}
    <h2>5. 制作备注</h2>${ul(D.g.v)}
    </div></div>
  </div>`;
  $('#inFoldHead').onclick = ev => {
    if (ev.target.closest('[data-mock]')) return;
    state.inputOpen = !state.inputOpen;
    $('#inFold').classList.toggle('open', state.inputOpen);
  };
  $('#docFoldHead').onclick = ev => {
    if (ev.target.closest('[data-mock]')) return;
    state.docOpen = !state.docOpen;
 $('#docFold').classList.toggle('open', state.docOpen);
  };
}
