/* ---------- AI 生成模型清单（对齐竞品能力面板） ---------- */
const GEN_MODELS = {
  image: [
    { id: 'gpt-image-2', ic: '◍', n: 'GPT Image 2', d: '4K 画面，文字与 UI 控制精准', cost: 10 },
    { id: 'nano-banana-2', ic: '🍌', n: 'Nano Banana 2', d: '细节扎实，成片分镜', cost: 8 },
    { id: 'grok-imagine', ic: '✧', n: 'Grok Imagine Image', d: '创意出图，真实生动', cost: 8 },
    { id: 'seedream-5-pro', ic: '▮', n: 'Seedream 5.0 Pro', d: '旗舰画质，专业成片', cost: 16 },
    { id: 'seedream-5', ic: '▮', n: 'Seedream 5.0', d: '画质升级，细节丰富', cost: 12 },
    { id: 'gpt-image-25-sunburst', ic: '◍', n: 'GPT Image 2.5 Sunburst', d: '精准打磨角色与场景画面', cost: 14 },
    { id: 'gpt-image-25-flare', ic: '◍', n: 'GPT Image 2.5 Flare', d: '快速探索剧情镜头方案', cost: 6 }
  ],
  video: [
    { id: 'seedance-2', ic: '◈', n: 'Seedance 2.0', d: '镜头运动自然，人物稳定', cost: 60 },
    { id: 'kling-v3-pro', ic: '◆', n: 'Kling v3 Pro', d: '旗舰画质，长镜头一致性', cost: 90 },
    { id: 'kling-v3', ic: '◆', n: 'Kling v3', d: '性价比首选，出片快', cost: 45 },
    { id: 'veo-3', ic: '▷', n: 'Veo 3', d: '原生同步音频，情绪充沛', cost: 120 }
  ]
};
const IMG_RES = ['low', 'medium', 'high'];
const VID_RES = ['480p', '720p', '1080p'];
const VID_DUR = [4, 6, 8, 10];
const VID_MODE = [['t2v', '文生视频'], ['i2v', '图生视频'], ['ff2lf', '首尾帧']];

/** 元素当前生成配置（模态内可改，跨开关记忆） */
const GEN_CFG = {
  image: { model: 'gpt-image-2', res: 'medium' },
  video: { model: 'seedance-2', res: '720p', dur: 4, mode: 't2v' }
};

/* ---------- 参考图池：从 story.json 真实分镜帧里按角色 / 场景反查 ---------- */
/** 元素名 → story.json 里的 char key */
const EL_CHAR_KEY = {
  '李傲天': ['aotian'], '沈婉儿': ['wanerh', 'waner'], '赵铁牛': ['zhao'],
  '李老憨': ['laohan'], '王婆': ['wangpo'], '粮贩子': ['vendor'], '货郎': ['vendor'],
  '副主任': ['fuzhuren'], '村民': ['villager'], '布铺老板娘': ['shopwoman']
};
/** 元素名 → 台词/标题里的提及词（char 字段拿不到画面时的第二路召回） */
const EL_MENTION = {
  '李傲天': ['傲天'], '沈婉儿': ['婉儿', '嫂子'], '赵铁牛': ['赵铁牛', '铁牛'],
  '李老憨': ['李老憨', '老憨'], '王婆': ['王婆', '媒人'], '粮贩子': ['粮贩', '贩子', '收粮'],
  '副主任': ['副主任', '公章', '主任']
};
/** 场景元素名 → story.json scene 字段候选 */
const EL_SCENE_KEY = {
  '土窑洞·傲天家': ['house', 'night'], '一亩好地·村外坡地': ['field', 'outside'],
  '县城布铺': ['city'], '傲天家院子': ['village', 'house'], '村口土路': ['village', 'trans']
};
/** 道具元素名 → 台词提及词 */
const EL_PROP_KEY = {
  '半碗糊糊': ['糊糊', '半碗', '碗'], '米白布衣裳': ['衣裳', '布', '棉袄'],
  '一把锄头': ['锄头', '锄'], '一坛酒': ['酒', '坛']
};
/** 参考图上限：最多 5 张（上传超出时拒绝并 toast） */
const REF_MAX = 5;
let REF_POOL = {};   // `${kind}:${i}` → [url,...]
function buildRefPool() {
  REF_POOL = {};
  if (!S || !S.nodes) return;

  /* 所有带画面的节点摊平成一张可检索表 */
  const rows = [];
  Object.keys(S.nodes).forEach(k => {
    const n = S.nodes[k], m = n.media; if (!m) return;
    const f = m.poster || (m.kind === 'image' ? m.file : ''); if (!f) return;
    rows.push({
      url: mediaUrl(f), char: n.char || '', scene: n.scene || '',
      hay: [n.text, n.speaker, n.title, n.label, m.label].filter(Boolean).join(' ')
    });
  });
  const push = (pool, u) => { if (u && pool.indexOf(u) < 0) pool.push(u); };

  /* 1) 角色：char 字段精确召回 → 台词提及召回 → 同场景兜底 */
  (ELEMENTS.chars || []).forEach((x, i) => {
    const base = x.name.split('·')[0];
    const keys = EL_CHAR_KEY[base] || [];
    const words = EL_MENTION[base] || [base];
    const pool = [x.img];
    rows.forEach(r => { if (keys.indexOf(r.char) >= 0) push(pool, r.url); });
    if (pool.length < REF_MAX) rows.forEach(r => { if (words.some(w => r.hay.indexOf(w) >= 0)) push(pool, r.url); });
    if (pool.length < REF_MAX) rows.some(r => { push(pool, r.url); return pool.length >= REF_MAX; });
    REF_POOL['chars:' + i] = pool.slice(0, REF_MAX);
  });

  /* 2) 场景：scene 字段精确召回 → 名字关键词兜底 */
  (ELEMENTS.scenes || []).forEach((x, i) => {
    const keys = EL_SCENE_KEY[x.name] || [];
    const words = x.name.replace(/[·、]/g, ' ').split(/\s+/).filter(w => w.length >= 2);
    const pool = [x.img];
    keys.forEach(sk => rows.forEach(r => { if (r.scene === sk) push(pool, r.url); }));
    if (pool.length < REF_MAX) rows.forEach(r => { if (words.some(w => r.hay.indexOf(w) >= 0)) push(pool, r.url); });
    if (pool.length < REF_MAX) rows.some(r => { push(pool, r.url); return pool.length >= REF_MAX; });
    REF_POOL['scenes:' + i] = pool.slice(0, REF_MAX);
  });

  /* 3) 道具：台词提及召回（道具通常没有独立 scene 字段） */
  (ELEMENTS.props || []).forEach((x, i) => {
    const words = EL_PROP_KEY[x.name] || [x.name];
    const pool = [x.img];
    words.forEach(w => rows.forEach(r => { if (r.hay.indexOf(w) >= 0) push(pool, r.url); }));
    if (pool.length < REF_MAX) rows.some(r => { push(pool, r.url); return pool.length >= REF_MAX; });
    REF_POOL['props:' + i] = pool.slice(0, REF_MAX);
  });
}

function renderElement() {
  const E = ELEMENTS;
  const card = (x, kind, i) => `<div class="ecard" data-el="${kind}:${i}">
    <div class="pic"><img src="${x.img}" loading="lazy"><span class="badge">${kind === 'chars' ? '立绘 / 三视图' : kind === 'scenes' ? '场景基准图' : '道具基准图'}</span></div>
    <div class="txt">
      <div class="hd"><h5>${esc(x.name)}</h5>
        <button class="more" data-menu="${kind}:${i}" title="更多">⋮</button></div>
      <div class="tagrow">${x.tags.map(t => `<span class="tag">${esc(t)}</span>`).join('')}</div>
      <div class="desc">${esc(x.desc)}</div>
    </div>
    <div class="emenu hide" data-mn="${kind}:${i}">
      <button data-act="edit">✎ 编辑${kind === 'chars' ? '角色' : kind === 'scenes' ? '场景' : '道具'}信息</button>
      <button class="del" data-mock>🗑 删除${kind === 'chars' ? '角色' : kind === 'scenes' ? '场景' : '道具'}</button>
    </div>
  </div>`;
  const sec = (title, note, kind, add) => `
    <div class="sec-head" style="margin-top:26px"><h4>${title}${note ? `<span class="muted" style="font-weight:400">（${note}）</span>` : ''}</h4>
      <div class="acts"><button class="btn sm" data-mock>＋ ${add}</button>${kind === 'chars' ? '<button class="btn sm" data-mock>⟳ 一键重生</button>' : ''}</div></div>
    <div class="egrid">${E[kind].map((c, i) => card(c, kind, i)).join('')}</div>`;

  $('#stage').innerHTML = `<div class="pane wide">
    <div class="notice">元素由 step1 剧本自动拆解：角色 ${E.chars.length} · 场景 ${E.scenes.length} · 道具 ${E.props.length}。<b>点击任意卡片进入详情页</b>可查看基准图 / 改写提示词 / 重新生成。</div>
    ${sec('角色列表', '立绘 + 三视图为一致性锚，后续所有分镜图逐字复述角色卡字段', 'chars', '添加角色')}
    ${sec('场景列表', '', 'scenes', '添加场景')}
    ${sec('道具列表', '碎片承载物：关系 / 事实 / 旧案', 'props', '添加道具')}
  </div>`;

  const closeMenus = () => $('#stage').querySelectorAll('.emenu').forEach(m => m.classList.add('hide'));
  $('#stage').querySelectorAll('[data-menu]').forEach(b => b.onclick = ev => {
    ev.stopPropagation();
    const m = $(`[data-mn="${b.dataset.menu}"]`), open = !m.classList.contains('hide');
    closeMenus(); if (!open) m.classList.remove('hide');
  });
  $('#stage').querySelectorAll('.emenu [data-act="edit"]').forEach(b => b.onclick = ev => {
    ev.stopPropagation(); closeMenus();
    openElement(b.closest('.emenu').dataset.mn);
  });
  $('#stage').querySelectorAll('[data-el]').forEach(c => c.onclick = () => openElement(c.dataset.el));
  $('#stage').onclick = ev => { if (!ev.target.closest('.emenu,[data-menu]')) closeMenus(); };
}

/* ---------- 元素详情页（模态：基准图 + 变体条 + 提示词编辑 + 生成） ---------- */
function openElement(key) {
  const [kind, ix] = key.split(':');
  const x = ELEMENTS[kind][+ix];
  const list = ELEMENTS[kind];
  const kindName = kind === 'chars' ? '角色' : kind === 'scenes' ? '场景' : '道具';
  const refs = ((REF_POOL[key] && REF_POOL[key].length) ? REF_POOL[key] : [x.img]).slice(0, REF_MAX);
  REF_POOL[key] = refs;   // 上传后的改动持久化到池里，重开模态仍在
  let pick = 0;
  const old = $('#emodal'); if (old) old.remove();
  const wrap = el('div', 'emodal');
  wrap.id = 'emodal';
  wrap.innerHTML = `
    <div class="em-box">
      <div class="em-top">
        <h3>${esc(x.name)}</h3>
        <div class="em-tags">${x.tags.map(t => `<span class="tag">${esc(t)}</span>`).join('')}</div>
        <button class="em-x" id="emX">✕</button>
      </div>
      <div class="em-main">
      <div class="em-body">
        <div class="em-stage">
          <span class="em-pick">🔖 已选取 · <b id="emIx">1</b>/<span id="emStageCt">${refs.length}</span></span>
          <img src="${refs[0]}" id="emBig">
        </div>
        <div class="em-side">
          <button class="em-up ${refs.length >= REF_MAX ? 'dis' : ''}" id="emUp" title="上传参考图（最多 ${REF_MAX} 张）">⬆</button>
          <input type="file" id="emFile" accept="image/*" multiple hidden>
          <div class="em-slots" id="emSlots">
            ${refs.map((u, i) => `<button class="em-slot ${i === 0 ? 'on' : ''}" data-ri="${i}"><img src="${u}" loading="lazy"></button>`).join('')}
          </div>
          <span class="em-refct ${refs.length >= REF_MAX ? 'full' : ''}" id="emRefCt">${refs.length} / ${REF_MAX} 张参考</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      ${genPanel(x)}
      </div>
      <div class="em-nav"><span class="ct">${+ix + 1} / ${list.length} · ${kindName}</span></div>
      <button class="em-fab prev" id="emPrev" ${+ix === 0 ? 'disabled' : ''} title="上一个">‹</button>
      <button class="em-fab next" id="emNext" ${+ix === list.length - 1 ? 'disabled' : ''} title="下一个">›</button>
    </div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(wrap);
  bindGenPanel(wrap, x);

  /* 参考图槽位重绘 + 上限校验 */
  const slotHTML = (u, i) =>
    `<div class="em-slot ${i === pick ? 'on' : ''}" data-ri="${i}"><img src="${u}" loading="lazy">` +
    `${refs.length > 1 ? `<button class="em-del" data-rd="${i}" title="移除这张参考">✕</button>` : ''}</div>`;
  const syncRefs = () => {
    if (pick >= refs.length) pick = refs.length - 1;
    $('#emSlots').innerHTML = refs.map(slotHTML).join('');
    $('#emRefCt').textContent = `${refs.length} / ${REF_MAX} 张参考`;
    $('#emRefCt').classList.toggle('full', refs.length >= REF_MAX);
    $('#emUp').classList.toggle('dis', refs.length >= REF_MAX);
    $('#emIx').textContent = pick + 1;
    $('#emStageCt').textContent = refs.length;
    $('#emBig').src = refs[pick];
    bindSlots();
  };
  const bindSlots = () => {
    wrap.querySelectorAll('[data-ri]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
      pick = +b.dataset.ri;
      wrap.querySelectorAll('[data-ri]').forEach(y => y.classList.toggle('on', y === b));
      $('#emBig').src = refs[pick];
      $('#emIx').textContent = pick + 1;
    });
    wrap.querySelectorAll('[data-rd]').forEach(b => b.onclick = ev => {
      ev.stopPropagation();
      if (refs.length <= 1) { toast('⚠️ 至少保留 1 张参考图'); return; }
      refs.splice(+b.dataset.rd, 1);
      syncRefs();
      toast(`🗑 已移除 · 剩 ${refs.length}/${REF_MAX} 张`);
    });
  };
  syncRefs();

  // 上传参考图：单次 + 累计都不得超过 REF_MAX
  $('#emUp').onclick = () => {
    if (refs.length >= REF_MAX) { toast(`⚠️ 参考图最多 ${REF_MAX} 张，请先删掉一张再传`); return; }
    $('#emFile').click();
  };
  $('#emFile').onchange = ev => {
    const files = [].slice.call(ev.target.files || []);
    ev.target.value = '';
    if (!files.length) return;
    const room = REF_MAX - refs.length;
    if (room <= 0) { toast(`⚠️ 参考图最多 ${REF_MAX} 张`); return; }
    const bad = files.filter(f => !/^image\//.test(f.type));
    const big = files.filter(f => f.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024);
    if (bad.length) toast(`⚠️ 已跳过 ${bad.length} 个非图片文件`);
    if (big.length) toast('⚠️ 已跳过超过 8MB 的图片');
    const ok = files.filter(f => /^image\//.test(f.type) && f.size <= 8 * 1024 * 1024);
    if (!ok.length) return;
    if (ok.length > room) toast(`⚠️ 还能传 ${room} 张，多余的 ${ok.length - room} 张已忽略`);
    ok.slice(0, room).forEach(f => refs.push(URL.createObjectURL(f)));
    pick = refs.length - 1;
    syncRefs();
    toast(`✅ 已添加 ${Math.min(ok.length, room)} 张参考 · 共 ${refs.length}/${REF_MAX}`);
  };
  const close = () => { wrap.remove(); document.onkeydown = null; };
  $('#emX').onclick = close;
  wrap.onclick = ev => { if (ev.target === wrap) close(); };
  $('#emPrev').onclick = () => { if (+ix === 0) return; close(); openElement(`${kind}:${+ix - 1}`); };
  $('#emNext').onclick = () => { if (+ix === list.length - 1) return; close(); openElement(`${kind}:${+ix + 1}`); };
  document.onkeydown = ev => {
    if (ev.key === 'Escape') close();
    if (ev.target.tagName === 'TEXTAREA') return;
    if (ev.key === 'ArrowLeft') $('#emPrev').click();
    if (ev.key === 'ArrowRight') $('#emNext').click();
  };
}

/* ---------- 生成面板（提示词 + 模型 / 分辨率 / 时长选择器）---------- */
/** mode: 'image' | 'video'；video 面板多出「生成方式 / 时长」两个 chip */
function genPanel(x, mode) {
  mode = mode || 'image';
  const cfg = GEN_CFG[mode];
  const m = GEN_MODELS[mode].find(y => y.id === cfg.model) || GEN_MODELS[mode][0];
  const vChips = mode === 'video' ? `
    <button class="pchip gp" data-gp="mode">🎞 ${(VID_MODE.find(v => v[0] === cfg.mode) || VID_MODE[0])[1]} <i class="ca">▾</i></button>
    <button class="pchip gp" data-gp="dur">⏱ ${cfg.dur} 秒 <i class="ca">▾</i></button>` : '';
  return `
      <div class="em-prompt" data-mode="${mode}">
        <div class="em-ptop"><button class="em-add" data-mock>＋</button>
          <span class="em-plb">生成提示词 · 可直接改写</span></div>
        <textarea id="emPmt" spellcheck="false">${esc(x.pmt || x.vpmt || '')}</textarea>
        <div class="em-pbar">
          <button class="pchip gp" data-gp="model"><i class="mi">${m.ic}</i>${esc(m.n)} <i class="ca">▾</i></button>
          <button class="pchip gp" data-gp="res">◎ ${cfg.res} <i class="ca">▾</i></button>
          ${vChips}
          <span class="em-cost">🔥 <b id="emCost">${m.cost}</b></span>
          <button class="em-gen" data-mock="我是假的 等研发爸爸们做">✦ 生成</button>
        </div>
        <div class="gp-pop hide" id="gpPop"></div>
      </div>`;
}

/** 给生成面板里的 chip 绑定下拉浮层（模型 / 分辨率 / 时长 / 生成方式） */
function bindGenPanel(root, x) {
  const panel = root.querySelector('.em-prompt'); if (!panel) return;
  const mode = panel.dataset.mode || 'image';
  const cfg = GEN_CFG[mode];
  const pop = panel.querySelector('#gpPop');
  const closePop = () => { pop.classList.add('hide'); pop.innerHTML = ''; };

  const redraw = () => {
    const html = genPanel(x, mode);
    const tmp = el('div', '', html);
    const keep = panel.querySelector('#emPmt').value;
    panel.replaceWith(tmp.firstElementChild);
    root.querySelector('#emPmt').value = keep;
    bindGenPanel(root, x);
  };

  panel.querySelectorAll('[data-gp]').forEach(b => b.onclick = ev => {
    ev.stopPropagation();
    const t = b.dataset.gp;
    if (!pop.classList.contains('hide') && pop.dataset.t === t) { closePop(); return; }
    pop.dataset.t = t;
    if (t === 'model') {
      pop.innerHTML = `<div class="gp-hd">模型选择</div>` + GEN_MODELS[mode].map(m => `
        <button class="gp-it ${m.id === cfg.model ? 'on' : ''}" data-v="${m.id}">
          <i class="gi">${m.ic}</i>
          <span class="gt"><b>${esc(m.n)}</b><small>${esc(m.d)}</small></span>
          <span class="gc">🔥 ${m.cost}</span>
          <span class="gk">✓</span>
        </button>`).join('');
    } else if (t === 'res') {
      const opts = mode === 'video' ? VID_RES : IMG_RES;
      pop.innerHTML = `<div class="gp-hd">分辨率</div>` + opts.map(r => `
        <button class="gp-it sm ${r === cfg.res ? 'on' : ''}" data-v="${r}">
          <span class="gt"><b>${r}</b></span><span class="gk">✓</span></button>`).join('');
    } else if (t === 'dur') {
      pop.innerHTML = `<div class="gp-hd">视频时长</div>` + VID_DUR.map(d => `
        <button class="gp-it sm ${d === cfg.dur ? 'on' : ''}" data-v="${d}">
          <span class="gt"><b>${d} 秒</b></span><span class="gk">✓</span></button>`).join('');
    } else if (t === 'mode') {
      pop.innerHTML = `<div class="gp-hd">生成方式</div>` + VID_MODE.map(([v, lb]) => `
        <button class="gp-it sm ${v === cfg.mode ? 'on' : ''}" data-v="${v}">
          <span class="gt"><b>${lb}</b></span><span class="gk">✓</span></button>`).join('');
    }
    pop.classList.remove('hide');
    // 浮层贴到被点 chip 的左上方
    pop.style.left = Math.max(0, b.offsetLeft) + 'px';
    pop.querySelectorAll('[data-v]').forEach(it => it.onclick = e2 => {
      e2.stopPropagation();
      const v = it.dataset.v;
      if (t === 'model') cfg.model = v;
      else if (t === 'res') cfg.res = v;
      else if (t === 'dur') cfg.dur = +v;
      else if (t === 'mode') cfg.mode = v;
      closePop(); redraw();
    });
  });
  panel.onclick = ev => { if (!ev.target.closest('[data-gp],.gp-pop')) closePop(); };
}

/* =======================================================================
 * step3 分镜
 * ======================================================================= */
function renderShot() {
  const st = stats();
  const pend = st.total - st.framed;          // 完全无画面的节点
  const pendV = Math.max(0, st.choice - st.video);
  $('#stage').innerHTML = `<div class="sb">
    <div class="sb-side">
      <button class="aibtn" data-mock>✧ 智能续写</button>
      <div class="grp"><span>章节</span><span data-mock style="cursor:pointer">＋</span></div>
      <div class="proj-card"><img src="${PROJECT.cover}">
        <div><div class="t">${esc(PROJECT.title)}</div><div class="n">${st.chapters} 章 · ${st.total} 镜头</div></div></div>
      <div class="grp"><span>剧集</span>
        <span class="grp-act"><i id="epAll" title="全部展开 / 全部收起">⇕</i><i data-mock title="新增剧集">＋</i></span></div>
      <div class="epsearch"><input id="epQ" placeholder="搜镜头 / 台词…" value="${esc(state.epQ || '')}">
        ${state.epQ ? '<i id="epQX">✕</i>' : ''}</div>
      <div class="eptree" id="epTree"></div>
      <div class="iaabox">
        <div class="ih">💰 变现点分布<span>纯 IAA</span></div>
        <div class="ir"><span>广告解锁分叉</span><b>${st.ad}</b></div>
        <div class="ir"><span>免费主线分叉</span><b>${st.choice - st.ad}</b></div>
        <div class="ibar"><i style="width:${st.choice ? (st.ad / st.choice * 100).toFixed(1) : 0}%"></i></div>
        <button class="ijump" id="iaaJump">查看广告点清单 →</button>
      </div>
      <div class="foot">${st.chapters} 个章节 · ${st.total} 个镜头 · ${st.choice} 分叉 · ${st.ad} 广告点</div>
    </div>
    <div class="sb-main" id="sbMain"></div>
  </div>`;
  renderEpTree();
  state._pend = { img: pend, video: pendV };
  renderSbMain();
  const ij = $('#iaaJump'); if (ij) ij.onclick = openAdList;
}

/** 侧栏剧集树：每集独立折叠（默认全收起，仅当前集展开）+ 关键词过滤 + 一键展开/收起 */
function renderEpTree() {
  const tree = $('#epTree'); if (!tree) return;
  const q = (state.epQ || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const label = n => n.title || n.label || (n.media && n.media.label) || (n.text || '').slice(0, 12) || '';

  tree.innerHTML = S.chapters.map(c => {
    const ord = orderedNodes(c.id);
    const rows = ord.map((k, i) => ({ k, i, n: S.nodes[k] }))
      .filter(r => !q || (label(r.n) + ' ' + (r.n.text || '') + ' ' + r.k).toLowerCase().includes(q));
    if (q && !rows.length) return '';
    const open = q ? true : !!state.epOpen[c.id];
    const cur = state.chapter === c.id;
    return `<div class="epgrp ${open ? 'open' : ''} ${cur ? 'cur' : ''}">
      <div class="egh" data-ep="${c.id}"><span class="ca">▾</span>
        <img src="${mediaUrl(c.poster)}">
        <div class="t">${esc(c.title)}<small>${c.id} · ${ord.length} 镜头${q ? ' · 命中 ' + rows.length : ''}</small></div>
        <span class="cnt">${q ? rows.length : ord.length}</span></div>
      <div class="egb">${rows.map(r => {
      const mark = r.n.type === 'choice' ? '⑂' : r.n.type === 'end' ? '■' : '';
      return `<div class="si" data-jump="${r.k}"><span class="ix">${r.i + 1}</span><span class="tt">${mark ? mark + ' ' : ''}${esc(label(r.n) || r.k)}</span></div>`;
    }).join('')}</div>
    </div>`;
  }).join('') || '<div class="epempty">没有匹配的镜头</div>';

  tree.querySelectorAll('[data-ep]').forEach(d => d.onclick = () => {
    const id = d.dataset.ep;
    // 点非当前集：切章并展开；点当前集：折叠 / 展开
    if (state.chapter !== id) { state.chapter = id; state.epOpen[id] = true; renderShot(); return; }
    state.epOpen[id] = !state.epOpen[id];
    renderEpTree();
  });
  tree.querySelectorAll('[data-jump]').forEach(d => d.onclick = ev => {
    ev.stopPropagation();
    const id = d.dataset.jump;
    if (chapterOf(id) !== state.chapter) { state.chapter = chapterOf(id); state.epOpen[state.chapter] = true; renderShot(); }
    focusShot(id);
  });

  const all = $('#epAll');
  if (all) all.onclick = () => {
    const anyOpen = S.chapters.some(c => state.epOpen[c.id]);
    S.chapters.forEach(c => state.epOpen[c.id] = !anyOpen);
    renderEpTree();
  };
  const q0 = $('#epQ');
  if (q0) {
    q0.oninput = () => {
      state.epQ = q0.value;
      const at = q0.selectionStart;
      renderEpTree();
      const nq = $('#epQ'); if (nq) { nq.focus(); try { nq.setSelectionRange(at, at); } catch (_) { } }
    };
  }
  const qx = $('#epQX'); if (qx) qx.onclick = () => { state.epQ = ''; renderShot(); };
}

/** 广告点清单：全篇含广告解锁的分叉，点行直达该分镜 */
function openAdList() {
  const rows = [];
  S.chapters.forEach(c => orderedNodes(c.id).forEach(k => {
    const n = S.nodes[k];
    if (n.type !== 'choice') return;
    (n.options || []).forEach((o, i) => { if (o.ad) rows.push({ ep: c.id, k, i, o, title: n.title || k }); });
  }));
  const w = el('div', 'emodal');
  w.id = 'adModal';
  w.innerHTML = `<div class="em-box" style="width:min(880px,100%)">
    <div class="em-top"><h3>💰 广告点清单</h3>
      <div class="em-tags"><span class="tag k">纯 IAA · 共 ${rows.length} 处</span>
        <span class="tag">广告只解锁「额外的看见」，不出售胜利</span></div>
      <button class="em-x" id="adX">✕</button></div>
    <div class="adlist">${rows.map(r => `<div class="adrow" data-jump="${r.k}">
      <span class="ep">${r.ep}</span>
      <span class="nt">${esc(r.title)}</span>
      <span class="ot">${esc(r.o.text)}</span>
      <span class="to">→ ${esc(r.o.next || '—')}</span>
      <span class="go">定位</span></div>`).join('')}</div>
  </div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(w);
  const close = () => w.remove();
  $('#adX').onclick = close;
  w.onclick = ev => { if (ev.target === w) close(); };
  w.querySelectorAll('[data-jump]').forEach(r => r.onclick = () => {
    const k = r.dataset.jump; close();
    if (chapterOf(k) !== state.chapter) { state.chapter = chapterOf(k); renderShot(); }
    state.sbMode = 'script'; renderSbMain(); focusShot(k);
  });
}

/** 侧栏点镜头 → 主区滚动定位并高亮 */
function focusShot(id) {
  if (state.sbMode !== 'script') { state.sbMode = 'script'; renderSbMain(); }
  const tr = $('#epTree');
  if (tr) tr.querySelectorAll('.si').forEach(x => x.classList.toggle('on', x.dataset.jump === id));
  const t = $(`[data-node="${id}"]`); if (!t) return;
  t.classList.add('hl');
  setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove('hl'), 1600);
  try { t.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' }); } catch (_) { t.scrollIntoView(); }
}

function renderSbMain() {
  const m = $('#sbMain'); if (!m) return;
  const st = stats();
  const pd = state._pend || { img: 0, video: 0 };
  const done = st.framed === st.total;
  m.innerHTML = `<div class="sb-bar">
      <button class="batch ${pd.img ? '' : 'ok'}" data-mock>▣ 批量生成图片 · ${pd.img ? '剩余 ' + pd.img : '已完成'}</button>
      <button class="batch ${pd.video ? '' : 'ok'}" data-mock>▶ 批量生成视频 · ${pd.video ? '剩余 ' + pd.video : '已完成'}</button>
      <div class="hint ${done ? 'ok' : ''}" id="sbHint">
        ${done ? '✓ 全部 ' + st.total + ' 个节点画面已就绪（自有素材 ' + st.withMedia + ' · 视频 ' + st.video + ' · 沿用上镜 ' + (st.framed - st.withMedia) + '），可进入发布'
      : '⚠ 请先完成所有图片和视频生成，然后再发布游戏。'}
        <i class="cl" id="sbHintX">✕</i>
      </div>
      <div class="seg"><button data-m="script" class="${state.sbMode === 'script' ? 'on' : ''}">▤ 脚本模式</button><button data-m="flow" class="${state.sbMode === 'flow' ? 'on' : ''}">⋔ 流程模式</button></div>
    </div><div id="sbBody"></div>`;
  m.querySelectorAll('[data-m]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.sbMode = b.dataset.m; renderSbMain(); });
  const hx = $('#sbHintX'); if (hx) hx.onclick = () => $('#sbHint').remove();
  state.sbMode === 'script' ? renderScriptMode() : renderFlowMode();
}

/** scene 键 → 场景元素显示名（分镜卡 chip 与素材模态共用） */
const SCENE_NAME = { house: '土窑洞·傲天家', city: '县城布铺', end: '结局', field: '一亩好地·村外坡地', yard: '傲天家院子', village: '村口土路' };
function renderScriptMode() {
  const ep = state.chapter, chap = S.chapters.find(c => c.id === ep);
  const ord = orderedNodes(ep);
  /** 元素引用 chip：优先匹配 ELEMENTS 拿到缩略图 */
  const refChip = (name, pool) => {
    const hit = ELEMENTS[pool].find(e => e.name === name || e.name.split('·')[0] === String(name).split('·')[0]);
    return `<span class="ref"><i class="av">${hit ? `<img src="${hit.img}">` : '◍'}</i>${esc(hit ? hit.name : name)}</span>`;
  };
  let html = `<div class="epblock"><div class="eh"><img src="${mediaUrl(chap.poster)}">
      <div><div class="t">${esc(chap.title)}</div><div class="n">${ep} · ${ord.length} 个镜头</div></div>
      <button class="btn sm" style="margin-left:auto" data-mock>＋ 新建分镜</button>
      <button class="btn sm" data-mock>⌃</button></div>`;
  ord.forEach((k, i) => {
    const n = S.nodes[k], tk = transKind(n);
    const title = n.title || (n.media && n.media.label) || (n.type === 'end' ? n.title : '') || k;
    const spk = n.speaker ? `<span class="sp">${esc(n.speaker)}：</span>` : '';
    const own = nodePoster(n), poster = own || INHERIT[k];
    const chips = [];
    if (n.char && S.chars[n.char]) chips.push(refChip(S.chars[n.char].displayName, 'chars'));
    if (n.scene) chips.push(refChip(SCENE_NAME[n.scene] || n.scene, 'scenes'));
    if (n.media && n.media.kind === 'skillcard') chips.push(`<span class="ref k"><i class="av">◈</i>技能卡 ${esc(n.media.name || '')}</span>`);
    html += `<div class="shot" data-node="${k}">
      <div class="col">
        <div class="sh"><h6><span class="ix">${i + 1}</span>${esc(title)}</h6>
          <span class="ia"><i data-mock title="编辑分镜">✎</i><i class="dl" data-mock title="删除分镜">🗑</i></span></div>
        <div class="refs">${chips.join('')}
          ${n.media && n.media.kind === 'video' ? '<span class="ref t">▶ 视频 ' + (n.media.duration || '') + 's</span>'
        : own ? '<span class="ref t">▣ 图片</span>' : '<span class="ref t">↧ 沿用上镜画面</span>'}</div>
        <div class="lines" ${n.text ? `data-line="${k}" title="点击编辑台词"` : ''}>${n.text ? spk + esc(n.text) : '<span class="nar">（无台词 · 过场镜头）</span>'}</div>
        ${n.type === 'choice' ? `<div class="opts">${(n.options || []).map((o, oi) => `
          <div class="opt ${o.ad ? 'isad' : ''}" data-opt="${k}:${oi}">
            <span class="i">${o.icon === 'fight' ? '⚔' : o.icon === 'hide' ? '👁' : o.icon === 'say' ? '💬' : '›'}</span>
            <span class="otx" data-edit="${k}:${oi}" title="点击编辑选项文案">${esc(o.text)}</span>
            <button class="adsw ${o.ad ? 'on' : ''}" data-ad="${k}:${oi}" title="切换：广告解锁 / 免费">${o.ad ? '📺 广告解锁' : '🆓 免费'}</button>
            ${isDeadOpt(o) ? '<span class="detag">导向结局</span>' : (statLabel(o.stat) ? `<span class="st">${esc(statLabel(o.stat))}</span>` : '')}
            <span class="to" data-next="${k}:${oi}" title="点击修改跳转目标">→ ${esc(o.next)}</span></div>`).join('')}</div>` : ''}
        ${n.type === 'end' ? `<div class="opts"><div class="opt"><span class="i">■</span><span>结局类型：${(n.ending || '').startsWith('HE') ? '游戏结局（HE）' : '死亡结局（BE）'} · ${esc(n.ending || '')}</span><span class="to">复活：看广告 / 直接</span></div></div>` : ''}
      </div>
      <div class="trans ${tk.cls}">
        <div class="th"><b>${tk.icon} ${tk.label}</b><span class="ed" data-mock>编辑</span></div>
        <div class="jump">${n.type === 'choice' ? (n.options || []).length + ' 个分叉' : n.type === 'end' ? '章节 / 游戏结局' : '跳转到 → ' + esc(n.next || '—')}</div>
      </div>
      ${poster ? `<div class="frame" data-shot="${k}"><img src="${poster}" loading="lazy"><span class="fx">▣</span>${own && n.media.kind === 'video' ? '<span class="pill">▶ 首帧</span>' : own ? '' : '<span class="pill">↧ 沿用</span>'}</div>`
        : `<div class="frame empty" data-shot="${k}">待生成</div>`}
    </div>`;
  });
  html += '</div>';
  $('#sbBody').innerHTML = html;
  $('#sbBody').querySelectorAll('[data-shot]').forEach(f => f.onclick = ev => {
    ev.stopPropagation(); openShot(f.dataset.shot);
  });
  bindScriptEdits();
}

/* ---------- 脚本模式 · 真实编辑：选项文案 / 广告免费 / 跳转 / 台词 ---------- */
function bindScriptEdits() {
  const body = $('#sbBody'); if (!body) return;
  const pick = key => { const [k, oi] = key.split(':'); return { n: S.nodes[k], k, oi: +oi }; };

  /* ① 广告 / 免费 一键切换（写回 S.nodes，全站统计与发布页同步） */
  body.querySelectorAll('[data-ad]').forEach(b => b.onclick = ev => {
    ev.stopPropagation();
    const { n, k, oi } = pick(b.dataset.ad);
    const o = n.options[oi];
    o.ad = !o.ad;
    toast(`${k} 选项${oi + 1} 已设为「${o.ad ? '📺 广告解锁' : '🆓 免费'}」　纯 IAA 广告点：${stats().ad} 处`);
    renderShot(); focusShot(k);
  });

  /* ② 选项文案就地编辑（Enter 保存 / Esc 取消 / 失焦保存） */
  const inlineEdit = (span, get, set, k, label) => {
    span.onclick = ev => {
      ev.stopPropagation();
      if (span.querySelector('input')) return;
      const old = get();
      const ip = el('input', 'inl');
      ip.value = old;
      span.textContent = ''; span.appendChild(ip);
      ip.focus(); ip.select();
      let done = false;
      const save = ok => {
        if (done) return; done = true;
        const v = ip.value.trim();
        if (ok && v && v !== old) {
          set(v);
          buildInherit();
          toast(`${label} 已更新：${v.slice(0, 18)}${v.length > 18 ? '…' : ''}（预览同步生效）`);
        }
        renderShot(); focusShot(k);
      };
      ip.onkeydown = e2 => {
        e2.stopPropagation();
        if (e2.key === 'Enter') save(true);
        if (e2.key === 'Escape') save(false);
      };
      ip.onblur = () => save(true);
    };
  };
  body.querySelectorAll('[data-edit]').forEach(sp => {
    const { n, k, oi } = pick(sp.dataset.edit);
    inlineEdit(sp, () => n.options[oi].text, v => { n.options[oi].text = v; }, k, `${k} 选项${oi + 1} 文案`);
  });
  body.querySelectorAll('[data-line]').forEach(sp => {
    const k = sp.dataset.line, n = S.nodes[k];
    sp.onclick = ev => {
      if (ev.target.closest('input')) return;
      ev.stopPropagation();
      if (sp.querySelector('textarea')) return;
      const old = n.text;
      const ta = el('textarea', 'inl ta');
      ta.value = old;
      sp.textContent = ''; sp.appendChild(ta);
      ta.focus(); ta.select();
      let done = false;
      const save = ok => {
        if (done) return; done = true;
        const v = ta.value.trim();
        if (ok && v && v !== old) { n.text = v; toast(`${k} 台词已更新（预览同步生效）`); }
        renderShot(); focusShot(k);
      };
      ta.onkeydown = e2 => {
        e2.stopPropagation();
        if (e2.key === 'Enter' && !e2.shiftKey) { e2.preventDefault(); save(true); }
        if (e2.key === 'Escape') save(false);
      };
      ta.onblur = () => save(true);
    };
  });

  /* ③ 跳转目标改写：下拉选择同章节点（真实写回） */
  body.querySelectorAll('[data-next]').forEach(sp => sp.onclick = ev => {
    ev.stopPropagation();
    const { n, k, oi } = pick(sp.dataset.next);
    const ord = orderedNodes(state.chapter).filter(x => x !== k);
    const sel = el('select', 'inl sel');
    sel.innerHTML = ord.map(x => {
      const t = S.nodes[x].title || (S.nodes[x].text || '').slice(0, 12) || x;
      return `<option value="${x}" ${x === n.options[oi].next ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(x)} · ${esc(t)}</option>`;
    }).join('');
    sp.textContent = ''; sp.appendChild(sel);
    sel.focus();
    sel.onchange = () => {
      n.options[oi].next = sel.value;
      buildInherit();
      toast(`${k} 选项${oi + 1} 跳转改为 → ${sel.value}（流程模式与预览同步）`);
      renderShot(); focusShot(k);
    };
    sel.onblur = () => { renderShot(); focusShot(k); };
  });
}

/* ---------- 分镜素材模态（图片 / 视频 双 Tab · 对齐竞品编排器） ---------- */
function openShot(id, tab) {
  const n = S.nodes[id]; if (!n) return;
  tab = tab || ((n.media && n.media.kind === 'video') ? 'video' : 'image');
  const ord = orderedNodes(state.chapter);
  const pos = ord.indexOf(id);
  const title = n.title || (n.media && n.media.label) || id;
  const own = nodePoster(n), poster = own || INHERIT[id];
  const isVideo = !!(n.media && n.media.kind === 'video');

  // 参考图池：本镜画面 + 同角色 / 同场景其余画面（真素材，非占位）
  const pool = [];
  if (poster) pool.push(poster);
  ord.forEach(k => { const p = frameOf(k); if (p && !pool.includes(p)) pool.push(p); });
  const refs = pool.slice(0, REF_MAX);

  // 提示词：图片走画面描述，视频走「开场状态快照 + 动作」
  const sceneName = SCENE_NAME[n.scene] || n.scene || '';
  const charName = (n.char && S.chars[n.char] && S.chars[n.char].displayName) || '';
  const basePmt = [
    charName ? `主体：${charName}` : '',
    sceneName ? `场景：${sceneName}` : '',
    n.text ? `台词：${n.text}` : '（无台词 · 过场镜头）'
  ].filter(Boolean).join('\n');
  const shotX = {
    pmt: `${basePmt}\n构图：竖屏 9:16 平视中近景，人物占画面主要面积。\n光线：与场景基准图一致的自然光。\n${'要求排除：多画面布局、宫格、分屏、边框、文字、字幕、logo、水印。'}`,
    vpmt: `开场状态快照：${basePmt}\n运动：镜头轻微推近，人物完成一次自然的情绪转变，动作幅度克制。\n收尾：停在可作为下一镜首帧的静止姿态。`
  };

  const old = $('#emodal'); if (old) old.remove();
  const wrap = el('div', 'emodal shotmodal');
  wrap.id = 'emodal';
  wrap.innerHTML = `
    <div class="em-box">
      <div class="em-top">
        <h3><span class="sm-ix">${pos + 1}</span>${esc(title)}</h3>
        <div class="sm-tabs">
          <button class="${tab === 'image' ? 'on' : ''}" data-tab="image">图片</button>
          <button class="${tab === 'video' ? 'on' : ''}" data-tab="video">视频</button>
        </div>
        <button class="em-x" id="emX">✕</button>
      </div>
      <div class="em-main">
      <div class="em-body">
        <div class="em-stage">
          ${tab === 'image'
      ? (poster ? `<span class="em-pick">${isVideo ? '🎬 视频首帧' : '🖼 本镜画面'}</span><img src="${poster}" id="emBig">`
        : `<div class="sm-empty">画面待生成</div>`)
      : (isVideo ? `<span class="em-pick">▶ 已生成 ${(n.media.duration || 4)}s</span><img src="${poster}" id="emBig">`
        : `<div class="sm-empty">视频待生成</div>`)}
        </div>
        <div class="em-side">
          <button class="em-up ${tab === 'image' && refs.length >= REF_MAX ? 'dis' : ''}" id="emUp" title="上传参考图（最多 ${REF_MAX} 张）">⬆</button>
          <input type="file" id="emFile" accept="image/*" multiple hidden>
          ${tab === 'image' ? `<div class="em-slots" id="emSlots">${refs.map((u, i) => `
            <button class="em-slot ${i === 0 ? 'on' : ''}" data-ri="${i}"><img src="${u}" loading="lazy"></button>`).join('')}</div>
          <span class="em-refct ${refs.length >= REF_MAX ? 'full' : ''}" id="emRefCt">${refs.length} / ${REF_MAX} 张参考</span>` : ''}
        </div>
      </div>
      ${genPanel(shotX, tab === 'video' ? 'video' : 'image')}
      </div>
      <div class="em-nav"><span class="ct">${pos + 1} / ${ord.length} · ${esc(state.chapter)} 分镜</span></div>
      <button class="em-fab prev" id="emPrev" ${pos <= 0 ? 'disabled' : ''} title="上一镜">‹</button>
      <button class="em-fab next" id="emNext" ${pos >= ord.length - 1 ? 'disabled' : ''} title="下一镜">›</button>
    </div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(wrap);
  if (tab === 'video') { const t = wrap.querySelector('#emPmt'); if (t) t.value = shotX.vpmt; }
  bindGenPanel(wrap, tab === 'video' ? { pmt: shotX.vpmt } : shotX);

  const close = () => { wrap.remove(); document.onkeydown = null; };
  $('#emX').onclick = close;
  wrap.onclick = ev => { if (ev.target === wrap) close(); };
  wrap.querySelectorAll('[data-tab]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { close(); openShot(id, b.dataset.tab); });
  let spick = 0;
  const sSlot = (u, i) =>
    `<div class="em-slot ${i === spick ? 'on' : ''}" data-ri="${i}"><img src="${u}" loading="lazy">` +
    `${refs.length > 1 ? `<button class="em-del" data-rd="${i}" title="移除这张参考">✕</button>` : ''}</div>`;
  const redrawSlots = () => {
    const slots = $('#emSlots'); if (!slots) return;
    if (spick >= refs.length) spick = refs.length - 1;
    slots.innerHTML = refs.map(sSlot).join('');
    $('#emRefCt').textContent = `${refs.length} / ${REF_MAX} 张参考`;
    $('#emRefCt').classList.toggle('full', refs.length >= REF_MAX);
    $('#emUp').classList.toggle('dis', refs.length >= REF_MAX);
    const big = $('#emBig'); if (big) big.src = refs[spick];
    bindSlots();
  };
  const bindSlots = () => {
    wrap.querySelectorAll('[data-ri]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
      spick = +b.dataset.ri;
      wrap.querySelectorAll('[data-ri]').forEach(y => y.classList.toggle('on', y === b));
      const big = $('#emBig'); if (big) big.src = refs[spick];
    });
    wrap.querySelectorAll('[data-rd]').forEach(b => b.onclick = ev => {
      ev.stopPropagation();
      if (refs.length <= 1) { toast('⚠️ 至少保留 1 张参考图'); return; }
      refs.splice(+b.dataset.rd, 1);
      redrawSlots();
      toast(`🗑 已移除 · 剩 ${refs.length}/${REF_MAX} 张`);
    });
  };
  bindSlots();
  // 上传参考图（同样受 REF_MAX 约束）
  $('#emUp').onclick = () => {
    if (tab !== 'image') { toast('⚠️ 视频模式请在图片页签上传参考帧'); return; }
    if (refs.length >= REF_MAX) { toast(`⚠️ 参考图最多 ${REF_MAX} 张，请先删掉一张再传`); return; }
    $('#emFile').click();
  };
  $('#emFile').onchange = ev => {
    const files = [].slice.call(ev.target.files || []); ev.target.value = '';
    const room = REF_MAX - refs.length;
    if (room <= 0) { toast(`⚠️ 参考图最多 ${REF_MAX} 张`); return; }
    const ok = files.filter(f => /^image\//.test(f.type) && f.size <= 8 * 1024 * 1024);
    if (files.length && !ok.length) { toast('⚠️ 仅支持 8MB 以内的图片'); return; }
    if (ok.length > room) toast(`⚠️ 还能传 ${room} 张，多余的已忽略`);
    ok.slice(0, room).forEach(f => refs.push(URL.createObjectURL(f)));
    spick = refs.length - 1;
    redrawSlots();
    toast(`✅ 已添加 ${Math.min(ok.length, room)} 张参考 · 共 ${refs.length}/${REF_MAX}`);
  };
  if (tab === 'image') redrawSlots();
  $('#emPrev').onclick = () => { if (pos <= 0) return; close(); openShot(ord[pos - 1], tab); };
  $('#emNext').onclick = () => { if (pos >= ord.length - 1) return; close(); openShot(ord[pos + 1], tab); };
  document.onkeydown = ev => {
    if (ev.key === 'Escape') close();
    if (ev.target.tagName === 'TEXTAREA') return;
    if (ev.key === 'ArrowLeft') $('#emPrev').click();
    if (ev.key === 'ArrowRight') $('#emNext').click();
  };
}

/* ---------- 流程模式（竖版卡 · 可拖拽 · 选项直显 · 连线可改） ---------- */
const FLOW_W = 208, FLOW_HEAD = 32, FLOW_THUMB = 292, FLOW_META = 24, FLOW_OPT = 28, FLOW_GAPY = 54, FLOW_GAPX = 36;
function flowPosMap() {
  state.flowPos = state.flowPos || {};
  state.flowPos[state.chapter] = state.flowPos[state.chapter] || {};
  return state.flowPos[state.chapter];
}
function flowNodeH(k) {
  const n = S.nodes[k];
  const opts = n.type === 'choice' ? (n.options || []).length : 1;
  return FLOW_HEAD + (frameOf(k) ? FLOW_THUMB : 74) + FLOW_META + opts * FLOW_OPT + 10;
}
/** 出端口坐标：自然/结局节点一个底部端口；选项节点每个选项一个端口 */
function flowPortXY(pos, k, oi) {
  const p = pos[k]; if (!p) return null;
  const n = S.nodes[k];
  const opts = n.type === 'choice' ? (n.options || []).length : 1;
  const base = flowNodeH(k) - 10 - opts * FLOW_OPT;
  return { x: p.x + FLOW_W - 6, y: p.y + base + oi * FLOW_OPT + FLOW_OPT / 2 };
}
function flowInXY(pos, k) { const p = pos[k]; return p ? { x: p.x - 6, y: p.y + FLOW_HEAD / 2 } : null; }

function renderFlowMode() {
  const ep = state.chapter, ord = orderedNodes(ep);
  const inEp = new Set(ord);
  const chap = S.chapters.find(c => c.id === ep);
  const saved = flowPosMap();

  /* 自动分层：章节内 BFS depth，同层横向铺开；用户拖过的节点用保存坐标 */
  const depth = {}, order = [];
  const q = [[chap.start, 0]], seen = new Set();
  while (q.length) {
    const [id, d] = q.shift();
    if (!id || !S.nodes[id] || seen.has(id) || !inEp.has(id)) continue;
    seen.add(id); depth[id] = d; order.push(id);
    const n = S.nodes[id];
    (n.type === 'choice' ? (n.options || []).map(o => o.next) : [n.next]).forEach(x => { if (x && S.nodes[x]) q.push([x, d + 1]); });
  }
  ord.forEach(k => { if (!(k in depth)) { depth[k] = (Math.max(0, ...Object.values(depth)) + 1); order.push(k); } });
  const byDepth = {};
  order.forEach(k => { (byDepth[depth[k]] = byDepth[depth[k]] || []).push(k); });
  const pos = {};
  // 主线向右推进（depth → x 列），同层兄弟纵向堆叠（分叉向下展开）
  let x = 34;
  Object.keys(byDepth).map(Number).sort((a, b) => a - b).forEach(d => {
    const col = byDepth[d];
    let cy = 34;
    col.forEach(k => {
      pos[k] = saved[k] ? { ...saved[k] } : { x, y: cy };
      cy += flowNodeH(k) + FLOW_GAPY;
    });
    x += FLOW_W + FLOW_GAPX + 18;
  });
  const W = Math.max(920, Math.max(...order.map(k => pos[k].x)) + FLOW_W + 80);
  const H = Math.max(520, Math.max(...order.map(k => pos[k].y + flowNodeH(k))) + 90);

  /* 连线（每条带 from / 端口序号 / 目标） */
  const edges = [];
  order.forEach(k => {
    const n = S.nodes[k];
    const outs = n.type === 'choice'
      ? (n.options || []).map((o, i) => ({ oi: i, to: o.next, ad: !!o.ad, die: isDeadOpt(o) }))
      : [{ oi: 0, to: n.next }];
    outs.forEach(o => { if (o.to && pos[o.to]) edges.push({ from: k, ...o }); });
  });
  const orphan = order.filter(k => k !== chap.start && !edges.some(e => e.to === k)).length;
  const OPT_ICON = { fight: '⚔', hide: '👁', say: '💬' };

  const card = k => {
    const n = S.nodes[k], tk = transKind(n), p = pos[k];
    const t = n.title || (n.media && n.media.label) || (n.text || '').slice(0, 14) || k;
    const poster = frameOf(k);
    const own = nodePoster(n);
    const rows = n.type === 'choice'
      ? (n.options || []).map((o, i) => `<div class="fo ${o.ad ? 'ad' : ''} ${isDeadOpt(o) ? 'die' : ''}">
          <span class="oi">${OPT_ICON[o.icon] || '›'}</span>
          <span class="ot" title="${esc(o.text)}">${esc(o.text)}</span>
          ${o.ad ? '<span class="ab">AD</span>' : ''}
          <span class="og" title="下一步：${esc(o.next || '未连接')}">→ ${esc(o.next || '未连接')}</span>
          <i class="port" data-from="${k}" data-oi="${i}" title="拖到其他节点改连线"></i></div>`).join('')
      : `<div class="fo ${n.type === 'end' ? 'end' : 'nat'}">
          <span class="oi">${n.type === 'end' ? '■' : '─○'}</span>
          <span class="ot">${n.type === 'end' ? '结局 ' + esc(n.ending || '') : '自然过场'}</span>
          <span class="og" title="下一步：${esc(n.next || '无')}">→ ${esc(n.next || '章节结束')}</span>
          ${n.type === 'end' ? '' : `<i class="port" data-from="${k}" data-oi="0" title="拖到其他节点改连线"></i>`}</div>`;
    return `<div class="fnode t-${tk.k}" style="left:${p.x}px;top:${p.y}px;width:${FLOW_W}px" data-node="${k}">
      <div class="fh" data-drag="${k}">
        <b title="${esc(t)}">${tk.k === 'choice' || tk.k === 'ad' ? '◆ ' : tk.k === 'end' ? '■ ' : ''}${esc(t)}</b>
        <i class="del" data-del="${k}" title="删除节点">🗑</i>
      </div>
      ${poster ? `<div class="fthumb"><img src="${poster}" loading="lazy">
            ${own && n.media.kind === 'video' ? '<span class="tp">▶ 视频</span>' : own ? '' : '<span class="tp">↧ 沿用</span>'}</div>`
      : `<div class="fthumb empty">待生成</div>`}
      <div class="fm"><span class="id">${k}</span>${n.type === 'choice' ? `<span class="bn">${n.options.length} 叉</span>` : ''}</div>
      <div class="fopts">${rows}</div>
      <i class="inport"></i>
    </div>`;
  };

  $('#sbBody').innerHTML = `<div class="flow tool-${state.flowTool}" id="flowWrap">
    <div class="legend">
      <div class="toolseg" id="flowTool">
        <button data-t="pan" class="${state.flowTool === 'pan' ? 'on' : ''}" title="拖拽预览：按住画布任意处平移">✋ 拖拽</button>
        <button data-t="select" class="${state.flowTool === 'select' ? 'on' : ''}" title="点击编辑：移动卡片 / 改连线 / 点开分镜">➚ 点击</button>
      </div>
      <span><i class="lg nat"></i>自然</span>
      <span><i class="lg choice"></i>分叉</span>
      <span><i class="lg ad"></i>广告</span>
      <span><i class="lg end"></i>结局</span>
      <span class="muted">${ep} · ${order.length} 节点 / ${edges.length} 连线 · 孤儿 ${orphan}</span>
      <span class="tipx">${state.flowTool === 'pan' ? '✋ 按住画布空白处拖动即可平移全局，滚轮缩放' : '✋ 拖标题移卡片　⇢ 拖选项圆点到另一张卡改连线（真实生效）'}</span>
      <button class="rst" id="flowReset">⟲ 重排布局</button>
    </div>
    <div class="scaler" id="flowScaler" style="width:${W}px;height:${H}px">
    <div class="inner" id="flowInner" style="width:${W}px;height:${H}px">
      <svg id="flowSvg" width="${W}" height="${H}"></svg>
      ${order.map(card).join('')}
    </div></div>
    <div class="zoombar">
      <button data-z="-1">－</button><b id="flowZ">${Math.round(state.flowZoom * 100)}%</b><button data-z="1">＋</button>
      <button data-z="0" title="适应宽度">⤢</button>
    </div></div>`;
  /* 缩放：整块 transform，不影响拖拽换算（拖拽时用同比例反算） */
  const applyZoom = () => {
    const z = state.flowZoom;
    const sc = $('#flowScaler');
    sc.style.transform = `scale(${z})`;
    sc.style.width = (W * z) + 'px';
    sc.style.height = (H * z) + 'px';
    $('#flowZ').textContent = Math.round(z * 100) + '%';
  };

  /* --- 连线绘制 --- */
  const svg = $('#flowSvg');
  const drawEdges = () => {
    const seg = e => {
      const a = flowPortXY(pos, e.from, e.oi), b = flowInXY(pos, e.to);
      if (!a || !b) return '';
      const dx = Math.max(38, Math.abs(b.x - a.x) * 0.45);
      return `M${a.x},${a.y} C${a.x + dx},${a.y} ${b.x - dx},${b.y} ${b.x},${b.y}`;
    };
    svg.innerHTML = edges.map(e => {
      const d = seg(e); if (!d) return '';
      const c = e.die ? '#E5484D' : e.ad ? '#E0932A' : '#8f83b5';
      return `<path d="${d}" fill="none" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="2" ${e.die ? 'stroke-dasharray="5 4"' : ''} opacity=".85"/>
        <circle cx="${flowInXY(pos, e.to).x}" cy="${flowInXY(pos, e.to).y}" r="3.2" fill="${c}"/>`;
    }).join('') + '<path id="flowGhost" fill="none" stroke="#FF4D6D" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-dasharray="6 4"/>';
  };
  drawEdges();

  /* --- 拖拽移动卡片：位置持久化 + 连线实时跟随（仅点击模式） --- */
  const inner = $('#flowInner');
  const isPan = () => state.flowTool === 'pan';
  let drag = null;
  inner.querySelectorAll('[data-drag]').forEach(h => h.onmousedown = ev => {
    if (isPan() || ev.target.closest('[data-del]')) return;
    ev.preventDefault();
    const k = h.dataset.drag, node = h.closest('.fnode');
    drag = { k, node, sx: ev.clientX, sy: ev.clientY, ox: pos[k].x, oy: pos[k].y };
    node.classList.add('moving');
  });
  /* --- 拖端口改连线：真实写回 S.nodes（仅点击模式） --- */
  let wire = null;
  inner.querySelectorAll('.port').forEach(p => p.onmousedown = ev => {
    if (isPan()) return;
    ev.preventDefault(); ev.stopPropagation();
    wire = { from: p.dataset.from, oi: +p.dataset.oi };
    $('#flowWrap').classList.add('wiring');
  });
  const localXY = ev => { const r = inner.getBoundingClientRect(); const z = state.flowZoom; return { x: (ev.clientX - r.left) / z, y: (ev.clientY - r.top) / z }; };
  inner.onmousemove = ev => {
    if (drag) {
      const z = state.flowZoom;
      pos[drag.k] = { x: Math.max(6, drag.ox + (ev.clientX - drag.sx) / z), y: Math.max(6, drag.oy + (ev.clientY - drag.sy) / z) };
      drag.node.style.left = pos[drag.k].x + 'px';
      drag.node.style.top = pos[drag.k].y + 'px';
      drawEdges();
      return;
    }
    if (wire) {
      const a = flowPortXY(pos, wire.from, wire.oi), m = localXY(ev);
      const g = $('#flowGhost');
      if (a && g) g.setAttribute('d', `M${a.x},${a.y} C${a.x + 44},${a.y} ${m.x - 44},${m.y} ${m.x},${m.y}`);
      const t = ev.target.closest('.fnode');
      inner.querySelectorAll('.fnode').forEach(n => n.classList.toggle('drop', n === t && n.dataset.node !== wire.from));
    }
  };
  const endDrag = ev => {
    if (drag) {
      flowPosMap()[drag.k] = { ...pos[drag.k] };
      drag.node.classList.remove('moving');
      drag = null;
    }
    if (wire) {
      const t = ev.target.closest('.fnode');
      const to = t && t.dataset.node;
      $('#flowWrap').classList.remove('wiring');
      inner.querySelectorAll('.fnode').forEach(n => n.classList.remove('drop'));
      const w = wire; wire = null;
      if (to && to !== w.from) {
        const n = S.nodes[w.from];
        if (n.type === 'choice') n.options[w.oi].next = to; else n.next = to;
        buildInherit();
        toast(`已改连线：${w.from}${n.type === 'choice' ? ' 选项' + (w.oi + 1) : ''} → ${to}（脚本模式与预览同步生效）`);
        renderShot();
        return;
      }
      drawEdges();
    }
  };
  inner.onmouseup = endDrag;
  inner.onmouseleave = endDrag;

  /* --- 点开卡片跳转 / 删除（仅点击模式） --- */
  inner.querySelectorAll('.fthumb,.fm').forEach(d => d.onclick = ev => {
    if (isPan()) return;
    const k = ev.target.closest('.fnode').dataset.node; jumpTo(k);
  });
  inner.querySelectorAll('[data-del]').forEach(b => b.onclick = ev => {
    if (isPan()) return;
    ev.stopPropagation(); toast('删除节点 ' + b.dataset.del + '：我是假的 等研发爸爸们做');
  });
  const rs = $('#flowReset');
  if (rs) rs.onclick = () => { state.flowPos[state.chapter] = {}; renderFlowMode(); };

  /* --- 模式切换：拖拽（默认·小手平移全局）/ 点击（编辑） --- */
  const wrap = $('#flowWrap');
  $('#flowTool').querySelectorAll('[data-t]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
    state.flowTool = b.dataset.t; renderFlowMode();
  });
  /* 拖拽模式：按住画布任意处平移（含卡片上按下），滚轮缩放 */
  let pan = null;
  wrap.onmousedown = ev => {
    if (!isPan() || ev.button !== 0) return;
    ev.preventDefault();
    pan = { sx: ev.clientX, sy: ev.clientY, sl: wrap.scrollLeft, st: wrap.scrollTop };
    wrap.classList.add('grabbing');
  };
  wrap.onmousemove = ev => {
    if (!pan) return;
    wrap.scrollLeft = pan.sl - (ev.clientX - pan.sx);
    wrap.scrollTop = pan.st - (ev.clientY - pan.sy);
  };
  const endPan = () => { pan = null; wrap.classList.remove('grabbing'); };
  wrap.addEventListener('mouseup', endPan);
  wrap.addEventListener('mouseleave', endPan);
  wrap.onwheel = ev => {
    if (!(ev.ctrlKey || ev.metaKey || isPan() && ev.shiftKey)) return;
    ev.preventDefault();
    state.flowZoom = Math.max(0.25, Math.min(1.4, +(state.flowZoom - Math.sign(ev.deltaY) * 0.08).toFixed(2)));
    applyZoom();
  };

  /* --- 缩放条 --- */
  applyZoom();
  $('#sbBody').querySelectorAll('[data-z]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
    const d = +b.dataset.z;
    if (d === 0) {
      const vw = $('#flowWrap').clientWidth - 40;
      state.flowZoom = Math.max(0.25, Math.min(1, vw / W));
    } else state.flowZoom = Math.max(0.25, Math.min(1.4, +(state.flowZoom + d * 0.1).toFixed(2)));
    applyZoom();
  });
}

/* =======================================================================
 * step4 发布
 *   结构：① 游戏合成（闸门）→ ② 两个 Tab（测试预览 / 上架快手小游戏）
 *        ③ 主题模板 ④ 发布记录与历史版本
 * ======================================================================= */
const THEMES = {
  modern: { n: '现代风', desc: '都市写实 · 高饱和霓虹点缀', sw: ['#171a21', '#3b82f6', '#e2e8f0'] },
  ancient: { n: '古代风', desc: '宣纸质感 · 朱砂描金', sw: ['#2a1210', '#c0392b', '#e8c07d'] },
  cyber: { n: '赛博朋克风', desc: '暗底霓虹 · 故障描边', sw: ['#0b1020', '#7c5cff', '#22d3ee'] },
  girly: { n: '少女风', desc: '柔粉渐层 · 圆角贴纸', sw: ['#fff0f5', '#ff8fab', '#ffd6e0'], soon: 1 },
  wuxia: { n: '武侠风', desc: '水墨留白 · 竖排题字', sw: ['#1c1c1a', '#8c6239', '#d8d2c4'], soon: 1 },
  horror: { n: '悬疑风', desc: '低照度 · 冷绿噪点', sw: ['#0e1211', '#2f6b5a', '#9fb3ac'], soon: 1 }
};

/* 发布记录（demo 数据；一旦本次发布成功会 unshift 到最前） */
const RELEASES = [
  {
    v: 'v92.2', at: '2026-09-10 22:41', theme: 'modern', status: '已发布',
    points: ['成就入口点击穿透修复', 'EP04 限时抉择超时判定'],
    detail: '① achievements.js 增加 _entryVisible 命中门控与 setEntryVisible()，修复成就胶囊被隐藏后仍吃点击导致误触激励视频；② main.js 每帧同步 _achvVisible；③ EP04 3s 限时超时统一走 BE15，去掉旧的二次确认弹层。'
  },
  {
    v: 'v92.1', at: '2026-09-10 18:05', theme: 'modern', status: '已回滚',
    points: ['广告二次确认弹层（已否决）'],
    detail: '在所有 IAA 节点前插入「确认观看广告」二次弹层，评审认为打断叙事节奏、与「广告只解锁额外的看见」原则冲突，整体回滚，仅保留成就穿透修复。'
  },
  {
    v: 'v90.21', at: '2026-09-09 20:12', theme: 'ancient', status: '已发布',
    points: ['EP05 结局收束重写', '结局图鉴 18 条补齐'],
    detail: '① 重写 EP05「带不带全村」分野，拆出 HE1 / BE_ALONE 两条独立收束；② 结局图鉴补齐 18 条按代价命名的结局文案与说书人死因鉴定语音；③ 修正 3 处 choice 悬空引用。'
  }
];

function renderPublish() {
  const st = stats();
  const P = state.pub;
  const synthed = !!P.synthOk;
  const T = THEMES[state.theme];
  const assetsOk = st.framed === st.total;
  // 顶部 ①②③ 就是 tab 容器：只渲染当前选中那一段
  const cur = (!synthed && P.stage === 3) ? 1 : (P.stage || 1);
  P.stage = cur;

  $('#stage').innerHTML = `<div class="pane"><div class="pubflow">

    <!-- 阶段 tab（未解锁置灰 + 提示） -->
    <div class="pfsteps">
      ${[['①', '打包配置', '主题 · 素材 · 包信息', 1], ['②', '合成校验', '图谱 / 红线 / 体积', 2], ['③', '分发上线', '预览 / 上架 / 记录', 3]]
      .map(([i, t, sub, n]) => {
        const done = synthed && n <= 2;
        const on = cur === n;
        const lock = n === 3 && !synthed;
        return `<button class="pfs ${done ? 'done' : ''} ${on ? 'on' : ''} ${lock ? 'lock' : ''}"
          data-pfs="${n}" ${lock ? 'data-lockmsg="请先完成 ② 合成校验，全绿后自动解锁分发上线"' : ''}
          title="${lock ? '请先完成前序流程 · ② 合成校验' : '切换到 ' + t}">
          <span class="d">${lock ? '🔒' : done && !on ? '✓' : i}</span><b>${t}</b><small>${lock ? '需先完成 ②' : sub}</small></button>`;
      }).join('<i class="pfl"></i>')}
    </div>

    <div class="pfpanel">${cur === 1 ? paneConfig(P, T) : cur === 2 ? paneSynth(P, st, assetsOk, synthed) : paneDist(P, st, synthed)}</div>

  </div></div>`;

  /* --- 交互绑定 --- */
  $('#stage').querySelectorAll('[data-pfs]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
    if (b.classList.contains('lock')) { toast(b.dataset.lockmsg || '请先完成前序流程'); return; }
    P.stage = +b.dataset.pfs; renderPublish();
  });
  const nx = $('#pfNext'); if (nx) nx.onclick = () => { P.stage = 2; renderPublish(); };
  if (cur === 1) bindConfig(P);
  if (cur === 2) { const bs = $('#btnSynth'); if (bs) bs.onclick = runSynth; }
  if (cur === 3) {
    $('#stage').querySelectorAll('[data-ptab]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { P.tab = b.dataset.ptab; renderPublish(); });
    bindTabTest(); bindTabRelease(); bindHistory();
  }
}

      </div>

          <div class="field full"><label>隐私政策地址</label><input value="https://…/privacy.html"></div>

      </div>

      <!-- 主题：次要项，收成一行横向色板 -->
      <div class="cfg-sec minor">
        <div class="cfg-lb sm">UI 主题<small>只影响外壳配色，切换需重新合成</small></div>
        <div class="thmrow">
          ${Object.entries(THEMES).map(([k, t]) => {
        const on = state.theme === k;
        return `<button class="thm ${on ? 'on' : ''} ${t.soon ? 'soon' : ''}" data-theme="${k}" title="${t.n} · ${t.desc}">
              <span class="dots">${t.sw.map(c => `<i style="background:${c}"></i>`).join('')}</span>
              <span class="nm">${t.n}</span>${on ? '<span class="ck">✓</span>' : ''}
            </button>`;
      }).join('')}
        </div>
      </div>

    </div>
  </section>`;
}

/* ---------- ② 合成校验 ---------- */

  return `
  <section class="pfcard" id="synthCard">
    <div class="pfh static">
      <h4>② 合成校验</h4>
      <span class="pill ${synthed ? 'ok' : 'wait'}">${synthed ? '已合成 · ' + esc(P.synthAt) : '未合成'}</span>
      <span class="pfh-note">图谱校验 / 红线扫描 / 素材打包 / 体积门禁，一次跑完</span>
      <button class="btn pri" id="btnSynth">🗜 ${synthed ? '重新合成' : '开始合成'}</button>
    </div>
    <div class="pfb open">
      <div class="pubgrid">
        <div class="stat2 ${assetsOk ? '' : 'warn'}"><div class="n">${st.framed} / ${st.total}</div><div class="l">分镜画面就绪（自有 ${st.withMedia} · 沿用 ${st.framed - st.withMedia}）</div><div class="progress"><i style="width:${st.framed / st.total * 100}%"></i></div></div>
        <div class="stat2"><div class="n">${st.endings}</div><div class="l">结局节点（HE ${Object.values(S.endings).filter(e => e.type === 'HE').length} · BE ${Object.values(S.endings).filter(e => e.type !== 'HE').length}）</div><div class="progress"><i style="width:100%"></i></div></div>
        <div class="stat2"><div class="n">${st.choice}</div><div class="l">选项分叉节点</div></div>
        <div class="stat2"><div class="n">${st.ad}</div><div class="l">含广告解锁分叉（纯 IAA）</div></div>
      </div>
      <div class="chklist">
        ${[['图谱校验', `${st.total} 节点零孤儿 / 零死胡同 / 零 choice 悬空`, true],
      ['红线扫描', '涉政 0 / 涉黄 0 / 未成年 0 / 品牌 0', true],
      ['素材齐备', assetsOk ? `${st.total} 个节点画面全部就绪` : `${st.total - st.framed} 个节点缺画面（可合成但不建议发布）`, assetsOk],
      ['包体门禁', '主包 4.2MB ≤ 20MB', true]]
      .map(([t, d, ok]) => `<div class="chk ${ok ? 'ok' : 'warn'}"><i>${ok ? '✓' : '!'}</i><b>${t}</b><span>${d}</span></div>`).join('')}
      </div>
      <div class="loglines" id="synthLog">${P.synthLog ? esc(P.synthLog) : '等待合成…　点击右上「开始合成」'}</div>
    </div>
  </section>`;
}

    </div>
    <div class="pfb open">
      <div class="ptabs">
        <button data-ptab="test" class="${P.tab === 'test' ? 'on' : ''}"><i>🖥</i>测试预览</button>

        <button data-ptab="history" class="${P.tab === 'history' ? 'on' : ''}"><i>🕘</i>发布记录</button>
      </div>
      <div class="ptab-body">
        ${P.tab === 'history' ? renderHistory() : P.tab === 'test' ? tabTest(st) : tabRelease()}
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>`;
}

/* ---------- ① 的交互绑定 ---------- */
function bindConfig(P) {
  $('#stage').querySelectorAll('[data-theme]').forEach(d => d.onclick = () => {
    if (THEMES[d.dataset.theme].soon) { toast('该主题即将推出'); return; }
    if (state.theme === d.dataset.theme) return;
    state.theme = d.dataset.theme;
    if (P.synthOk) {
      P.synthOk = false;
      P.synthLog = `[theme] 已切换主题 → ${THEMES[state.theme].n}，需重新合成后方可预览 / 上架`;
      toast('主题已切换，需重新合成');
      renderShell(); return;
    }
    renderPublish();
  });
  // 素材：真实加载校验尺寸 + 点击看原图
  const SPEC = { cover: [640, 1400], logo: [148, 148] };
  $('#stage').querySelectorAll('[data-dim]').forEach(el => {
    const k = el.dataset.dim, url = P[k];
    if (!url) { el.textContent = '未上传'; el.className = 'as-badge warn'; return; }
    const im = new Image();
    im.onload = () => {
      const [ew, eh] = SPEC[k], fit = im.naturalWidth === ew && im.naturalHeight === eh;
      el.textContent = fit ? `✓ ${im.naturalWidth}×${im.naturalHeight}` : `⚠ ${im.naturalWidth}×${im.naturalHeight} · 需 ${ew}×${eh}`;
      el.className = 'as-badge ' + (fit ? 'ok' : 'warn');
    };
    im.onerror = () => { el.textContent = '加载失败'; el.className = 'as-badge warn'; };
    im.src = url;
  });
  $('#stage').querySelectorAll('[data-upbox]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
    const url = P[b.dataset.upbox]; if (url) lightbox(url);
  });
  $('#stage').querySelectorAll('[data-upload]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => toast('我是假的 等研发爸爸们做'));
  const ai = $('#inAppid');
  if (ai) ai.oninput = () => {
    P.appid = ai.value.trim();
    if (P.verified) { P.verified = false; P.uploaded = false; }
  };
  const nm = $('#inName'); if (nm) nm.oninput = () => { P.name = nm.value; };
  const nt = $('#inNote'); if (nt) nt.oninput = () => { P.note = nt.value; };
}

/* 原图灯箱 */
function lightbox(url) {
  const old = $('#lbx'); if (old) old.remove();
  const d = document.createElement('div');
  d.id = 'lbx'; d.className = 'lbx';
  d.innerHTML = `<div class="lbx-in"><img src="${esc(url)}"><button class="lbx-x">✕</button></div>`;
  d.onclick = ev => { if (ev.target === d || ev.target.classList.contains('lbx-x')) d.remove(); };
  document.body.appendChild(d);
}

/* ---------- 未合成时的闸门占位 ---------- */
function gateBlock() {
  return `<div class="gate">
    <div class="gicon">🔒</div>
    <div class="gt">尚未通过合成校验</div>
    <div class="gs">测试预览二维码与快手开放平台上架共用同一份合成产物（zip + 图谱校验 + 红线扫描）。<br>请先在上方 ② 点击「开始合成」，校验全绿后本区自动解锁。</div>
    <button class="btn pri" id="gateSynth" style="margin-top:14px">🗜 开始合成</button>
  </div>`;
}

      <div class="qrcap">快手 App 扫码试玩</div>
      <div class="qrmeta">${esc(S.version)} · ${THEMES[state.theme].n}<br>有效期 24 小时</div>
      <button class="btn sm" id="btnQrRefresh" style="margin-top:8px">⟳ 刷新二维码</button>
    </div>
    <div class="ptest-info">
      <div class="notice ok" style="margin:0 0 12px">✓ 测试包已生成 · 仅限白名单快手 ID 试玩，不进入线上分发</div>
      <div class="deliver">
        <div class="d"><div class="ic">🖥</div>浏览器完整交互预览<div class="muted" style="font-size:11px;margin-top:4px">右侧手机屏即为该预览</div></div>
        <div class="d" data-mock="我是假的 等研发爸爸们做"><div class="ic">🗜</div>代码 zip 包<div class="muted" style="font-size:11px;margin-top:4px">huangnianji-${esc(S.version)}.zip · 4.2MB</div></div>
        <div class="d" data-mock="我是假的 等研发爸爸们做"><div class="ic">📋</div>测试用例清单<div class="muted" style="font-size:11px;margin-top:4px">${st.choice} 个分叉 / ${st.endings} 个结局</div></div>
      </div>
      <div class="kv2" style="margin-top:14px">
        <div><dt>测试白名单</dt><dd>李某某、张某某 等 6 个快手 ID <span class="lnk" data-mock="我是假的 等研发爸爸们做">管理</span></dd></div>
        <div><dt>预览渠道</dt><dd>快手 App 扫码 / 浏览器 iframe 双通道</dd></div>
        <div><dt>数据上报</dt><dd>测试包埋点写入 sandbox 环境，不污染线上看板</dd></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>`;
}
function bindTabTest() {
  drawQR($('#qr'));
  const g = $('#gateSynth'); if (g) g.onclick = runSynth;
  const r = $('#btnQrRefresh'); if (r) r.onclick = () => { drawQR($('#qr'), Math.random() * 1e8 | 0); toast('二维码已刷新'); };
}

/* ---------- Tab 2 · 上架快手小游戏 ---------- */
function tabRelease() {
  const P = state.pub;
  const step = P.verified ? (P.uploaded ? 3 : 2) : 1;
  return `
  <div class="prel">
    <div class="flowsteps">
      ${[['1', '扫码验证快手 ID'], ['2', '上传开放平台'], ['3', '等待审核']]
      .map(([i, t], ix) => `<div class="fs ${step > ix + 1 ? 'done' : ''} ${step === ix + 1 ? 'on' : ''}"><span class="d">${step > ix + 1 ? '✓' : i}</span>${t}</div>`).join('<span class="fsline"></span>')}
    </div>

    <div class="relmeta">
      <span>包信息来自 ① 打包配置，无需重填</span>
      <b>AppID ${esc(P.appid)}</b><b>${esc(S.version)}</b><b>${THEMES[state.theme].n}</b><b>huangnianji-${esc(S.version)}.zip · 4.2MB</b>
      <button class="btn sm" id="btnBackCfg">✎ 回到 ① 修改</button>
    </div>

    <div class="verify ${P.verified ? 'ok' : ''}">
      <div class="vq">
        <div class="qr"><canvas id="qrAuth" width="29" height="29"></canvas></div>
        ${P.verified ? '<div class="vmask">✓</div>' : ''}
      </div>
      <div class="vtxt">
        <h5>${P.verified ? '快手 ID 校验通过' : '扫码验证快手 ID 管理权限'}</h5>
        ${P.verified
      ? `<p class="muted" style="font-size:12px">已授权账号 <b>快手ID 3xxxx917（李某某）</b> · 对 AppID <code>${esc(P.appid)}</code> 具备管理员权限，可调起开放平台上传接口。</p>
           <div class="tagrow"><span class="tag k">管理员</span><span class="tag">实名已认证</span><span class="tag">软著主体一致</span></div>`
      : `<p class="muted" style="font-size:12px">用 ① 中所填 AppID 的<b>管理员快手账号</b>扫码授权，平台确认上架权限后才能调起开放平台上传接口。</p>
           <ol class="vsteps"><li>快手 App 扫描左侧二维码</li><li>在 App 内点击「同意授权」</li><li>回到本页点击下方按钮</li></ol>`}
        <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;margin-top:10px">
          ${P.verified
      ? `<button class="btn sm" id="btnUnverify">切换账号</button><span class="muted" style="font-size:11px">授权有效期 30 分钟</span>`
      : `<button class="btn" id="btnVerify">✓ 我已扫码并授权</button><span class="muted" style="font-size:11px" id="vTip">等待扫码…</span>`}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="reline">
      <button class="btn pri" id="btnPublish" ${P.verified ? '' : 'disabled'}>🚀 上传到快手开放平台并提审</button>
      <span class="muted" style="font-size:12px">${P.verified ? '将上传 zip 包并调起提审，提审结果以开放平台后台为准' : '需先完成快手 ID 权限校验'}</span>
    </div>
    <div class="loglines" id="relLog" style="max-height:132px">${P.relLog ? esc(P.relLog) : '等待上传…'}</div>
  </div>`;
}

function bindTabRelease() {
  const P = state.pub;
  const bc = $('#btnBackCfg');
  if (bc) bc.onclick = () => { P.stage = 1; renderPublish(); };
  const qa = $('#qrAuth'); if (qa) drawQR(qa, 77341);
  const bv = $('#btnVerify');
  if (bv) bv.onclick = () => {
    if (!/^ks\d{15,20}$/.test(P.appid)) { toast('① 打包配置里的 AppID 不合法'); return; }
    const tip = $('#vTip'); if (tip) tip.textContent = '校验中…';
    bv.disabled = true;
    setTimeout(() => { P.verified = true; renderPublish(); toast('快手 ID 权限校验通过'); }, 900);
  };
  const bu = $('#btnUnverify'); if (bu) bu.onclick = () => { P.verified = false; P.uploaded = false; renderPublish(); };
  const bp = $('#btnPublish'); if (bp) bp.onclick = runPublish;
}

/* ---------- Tab 3 · 发布记录 / 历史版本 ---------- */
function renderHistory() {
  return `<div class="hist">
    <div class="notice ok" style="margin:0 0 14px">共 ${RELEASES.length} 条发布记录 · 每个历史版本可单独生成预览二维码回溯试玩</div>
    ${RELEASES.map((r, i) => `
      <div class="hrow ${state.pub.histOpen === i ? 'open' : ''}">
        <div class="hhead" data-hist="${i}">
          <span class="hv">${esc(r.v)}</span>
          <span class="hst ${r.status === '已发布' ? 'ok' : r.status === '已回滚' ? 'bad' : ''}">${esc(r.status)}</span>
          <span class="hat">${esc(r.at)}</span>
          <span class="hth">${THEMES[r.theme] ? THEMES[r.theme].n : r.theme}</span>
          <span class="hpts">${r.points.map(p => `<i>${esc(p)}</i>`).join('')}</span>
          <span class="hcar">${state.pub.histOpen === i ? '▴' : '▾'}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="hbody">
          <div class="hdetail"><dt>修改内容</dt><dd>${esc(r.detail)}</dd></div>
          <div class="hqr">
            <div class="qr"><canvas class="qrHist" data-seed="${1000 + i * 913}" width="29" height="29"></canvas></div>
            <button class="btn sm" data-histqr="${i}">⟳ 生成预览二维码</button>
            <span class="muted" style="font-size:11px">扫码试玩 ${esc(r.v)}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>`).join('')}
  </div>`;
}
function bindHistory() {
  $('#stage').querySelectorAll('[data-hist]').forEach(h => h.onclick = () => {
    const i = +h.dataset.hist;
    state.pub.histOpen = state.pub.histOpen === i ? -1 : i;
    renderPublish();
  });
  $('#stage').querySelectorAll('canvas.qrHist').forEach(c => drawQR(c, +c.dataset.seed));
  $('#stage').querySelectorAll('[data-histqr]').forEach(b => b.onclick = ev => {
    ev.stopPropagation();
    const row = b.closest('.hbody'); drawQR(row.querySelector('canvas.qrHist'), Math.random() * 1e8 | 0);
    toast('已生成该版本预览二维码');
  });
}

/* ---------- 合成 / 上传 ---------- */
function runSynth() {
  state.pub.synthOk = false;
  state.pub.synthLog = '';
  if (state.pub.tab === 'history') state.pub.tab = 'test';
  state.pub.stage = 2;   // 合成过程留在 ② 看日志
  renderPublish();
  const log = $('#synthLog');
  const st = stats();
  const lines = [
    `[build] theme=${state.theme} version=${S.version}`,
    `[check] 图谱校验 · BFS 可达 ${st.total}/${st.total} · 孤儿 0 · 死胡同 0 · choice 悬空 0`,
    `[check] 红线扫描 · 涉政 0 / 涉黄 0 / 未成年 0 / 品牌 0 → 全绿`,
    `[asset] 自有素材 ${st.withMedia} 个（视频 ${st.video}）· 沿用上镜 ${st.framed - st.withMedia} 个 · 全部走 CDN`,
    `[pack] 注入固定 UI 骨架（首页 / 章节列表 / 剧情页 / 结局页 / 音量·倍速·返回·分享）`,
    `[pack] 注入 IAA 广告节点 ${st.ad} 处 · 复活策略=看广告|直接`,
    `[pack] 主包 4.2MB（≤20MB 门禁通过）`,
    `[done] huangnianji-${S.version}.zip 已生成 · 测试预览与上架通道已解锁`
  ];
  log.textContent = '';
  let i = 0;
  const t = setInterval(() => {
    log.textContent += (i ? '\n' : '') + lines[i++];
    log.scrollTop = log.scrollHeight;
    if (i >= lines.length) {
      clearInterval(t);
      state.pub.synthOk = true;
      state.pub.synthAt = new Date().toLocaleString('zh-CN', { hour12: false }).replace(/\//g, '-');
      state.pub.synthLog = lines.join('\n');
      state.pub.stage = 3;   // 合成通过，自动进入 ③ 分发上线
      renderShell();         // 顶栏状态徽标同步
    }
  }, 300);
}
function runPublish() {
  const P = state.pub;
  if (!P.verified) return;
  const lines = [
    `[auth] appid=${P.appid} · 管理员校验通过（快手ID 3xxxx917）`,
    `[upload] huangnianji-${S.version}.zip → open.kuaishou.com/minigame/upload …`,
    `[upload] 分片 6/6 完成 · md5 校验一致`,
    `[submit] 调起提审接口 version=${S.version} theme=${state.theme} … OK`,
    `[submit] 提审单号 KSMG-2026091022-0417（demo）· 请在开放平台后台跟踪审核状态`,
    `[record] 已写入发布记录 · ${S.version}`
  ];
  P.relLog = '';
  const log = $('#relLog');
  log.textContent = '';
  let i = 0;
  const t = setInterval(() => {
    log.textContent += (i ? '\n' : '') + lines[i++];
    log.scrollTop = log.scrollHeight;
    if (i >= lines.length) {
      clearInterval(t);
      P.relLog = lines.join('\n'); P.uploaded = true; P.published = true;
      RELEASES.unshift({
        v: S.version + '-new', at: new Date().toLocaleString('zh-CN', { hour12: false }).replace(/\//g, '-'),
        theme: state.theme, status: '审核中',
        points: (P.note || '本次发布未填写更新说明').split(/[；;\n]/).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 3),
        detail: P.note || '（本次发布未填写更新说明）'
      });
      renderPublish(); toast('已提交提审 · 发布记录已更新');
    }
  }, 340);
}
/** 极简伪二维码（仅示意，非可扫码） */
function drawQR(cv, seedIn) {
  if (!cv) return;
  const N = 29, ctx = cv.getContext('2d');
  ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, N, N);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#111';
  let seed = (seedIn == null ? 20260910 : (seedIn | 0) || 1);
  const rnd = () => (seed = (seed * 1103515245 + 12345) & 0x7fffffff) / 0x7fffffff;
  for (let y = 0; y < N; y++) for (let x = 0; x < N; x++) if (rnd() > .52) ctx.fillRect(x, y, 1, 1);
  const eye = (ox, oy) => {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.fillRect(ox, oy, 8, 8);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#111'; ctx.fillRect(ox, oy, 7, 7);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.fillRect(ox + 1, oy + 1, 5, 5);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#111'; ctx.fillRect(ox + 2, oy + 2, 3, 3);
  };
  eye(0, 0); eye(N - 7, 0); eye(0, N - 7);
}

/** 左侧点分镜 → 通知真机 runtime 跳到该节点（跨 iframe 用 postMessage，iframe 内注入桥接） */
function jumpTo(id) {
  const n = S.nodes[id]; if (!n) return;
  state.pv.node = id;
  const fr = $('#rtFrame');
  const badge = $('#pvNode'); if (badge) badge.textContent = id;
  if (!fr || !fr.contentWindow) return;
  try { fr.contentWindow.postMessage({ __demo: 'goto', node: id }, '*'); } catch (_) { }
}
function pvStart(id) { jumpTo(id); }

/** iframe 里注入的桥（真机 runtime 只暴露 state / render，桥接层直接操作它们，不改真机代码） */
const RT_BRIDGE = `(function(){
  function G(){ return window.GameGlobal || window; }
  function findState(){ var g=G(); return g.state || null; }

  /* ── 平台预览：广告一律 mock 通过 ─────────────────────────────
   * 真机包内置 state.sessionAdBypass（kwai-ad.js _adminBypass 读它），
   * 打开后 KwaiAd.showRewarded 直接走 onRewarded，不改真机一行代码。
   * state 由 main.js 异步创建，所以持续轮询直到挂上，并防被重置覆盖。
   */
  var _adTimer = setInterval(function(){
    var st = findState();
    if (st && st.sessionAdBypass !== true) st.sessionAdBypass = true;
  }, 300);

  /* 兜底：万一某处绕过 bypass 直接调 SDK，也把激励视频短路成「已完播」 */
  var _adPatchTries = 0;
  var _adPatch = setInterval(function(){
    _adPatchTries++;
    var g = G(), Ad = g.KwaiAd || window.KwaiAd;
    if (Ad && Ad.showRewarded && !Ad.__demoMocked){
      Ad.__demoMocked = true;
      Ad.showRewarded = function(onRewarded){
        try { parent.postMessage({ __demo:'adMocked' }, '*'); } catch(_){}
        setTimeout(function(){ try{ onRewarded && onRewarded(); }catch(_){} }, 260);
      };
      clearInterval(_adPatch);
    }
    if (_adPatchTries > 80) clearInterval(_adPatch);
  }, 250);

  window.addEventListener('message', function(ev){
    var d = ev.data; if(!d || d.__demo !== 'goto') return;
    var g=G(), st=findState();
    if(!st){ return; }
    var STORY = g.STORY || window.STORY || {};
    var nodes = STORY.nodes || {};
    var node = nodes[d.node]; if(!node) return;
    // 关掉 splash / 转场 / 广告态，直接落到目标节点（等价真机「直通节点」）
    try{
      if (st.splash) st.splash.active = false;
      st.sessionAdBypass = true;
      st.chapterTransition = null;
      if (st.adLoading) st.adLoading.active = false;
      st.curId = d.node;
      if (node.ep) { st.ep = node.ep; st._activeChapter = node.ep; }
      if (node.epTitle) st.epTitle = node.epTitle;
      st.tw = { key:null, chars:0, startT:0, done:false };
      st.mediaImg = null; st.mediaImgKey = null; st.imgAdvKey = null;
      st.videoStarted = false;
      st.statsRevealed = true; st.statsRevealT = Date.now()-2000;
      st.sisterUnlocked = true; st.achvRevealed = true;
      st.hasPlayedStory = true;
      if (st.ui){ st.ui.tutorialSeen = true; st.ui.tutorialShowing = false; st.ui.logOpen=false; st.ui.paused=false; }
      // 章节解锁：所有章节直接放开，方便平台侧任意跳转预览
      try{
        var chs = (STORY.meta && STORY.meta.homeChapters) || [];
        var un = {}; chs.forEach(function(c){ un[c.id]=true; });
        localStorage.setItem('weihunqi_chapter_unlocks_v1', JSON.stringify(un));
      }catch(_){}
    }catch(_){}
    try { if (typeof g.render === 'function') g.render(); } catch(_){}
    try { parent.postMessage({ __demo:'gotoOk', node:d.node }, '*'); } catch(_){}
  });
  // 首帧就绪后回报，平台侧用来去掉 loading
  var tries=0;
  var t=setInterval(function(){
    tries++;
    var st=findState();
    if(st || tries>60){ clearInterval(t); try{ parent.postMessage({__demo:'ready', ok:!!st},'*'); }catch(_){} }
  }, 200);
})();`;

function renderPreview() {
  const box = $('#preview'); if (!box) return;
  const d = currentDevice();
  const scale = state.pv.scale || 1;
  box.style.setProperty('--devw', d.w + 'px');
  box.style.setProperty('--devh', d.h + 'px');
  box.style.setProperty('--devr', d.r + 'px');
  box.style.setProperty('--devb', d.b + 'px');
  // transform:scale 不改变布局盒尺寸 → 外包一层 .dev-wrap 占「缩放后」真实尺寸，
  // 机身以 top-left 为原点缩放贴进去，既不裁边也不留空
  const wrapW = Math.round((d.w + d.b * 2) * scale);
  const wrapH = Math.round((d.h + d.b * 2) * scale);
  box.style.setProperty('--pvw', (wrapW + 48) + 'px');
  box.innerHTML = `
    <div class="ph-bar"><span class="t">📱 实时预览 · 真机运行时</span>
      <span class="muted" style="font-size:11px" id="pvNode">${state.pv.node || '首页'}</span>
      <span class="r"><button class="btn sm" id="pvReload">⟳ 重载</button></span></div>
    <div class="ph-bar2">
      <span>机型</span>
      <select id="pvDev">${Object.entries(DEVICES).map(([k, v]) =>
      `<option value="${k}" ${state.pv.device === k ? 'selected' : ''}>${v.n} · ${v.w}×${v.h}</option>`).join('')}</select>
      <select id="pvScale">${SCALES.map(([v, lb]) =>
        `<option value="${v}" ${Math.abs(scale - v) < 1e-6 ? 'selected' : ''}>${lb}</option>`).join('')}</select>
      <span class="real">${d.w}×${d.h} @${d.dpr}x</span>
    </div>
    <div class="dev-wrap" style="width:${wrapW}px;height:${wrapH}px">
      <div class="device" style="transform:scale(${scale})">
        <i class="side si"></i><i class="side vu"></i><i class="side vd"></i><i class="side pw"></i>
        ${d.top === 'island' ? '<div class="island"></div>' : d.top === 'notch' ? '<div class="island notch"></div>' : ''}
        <div class="stage">
          <iframe id="rtFrame" src="${RUNTIME_URL}" allow="autoplay; fullscreen"
            title="荒年记 · 真机运行时预览"></iframe>
        </div>
        ${d.r > 24 ? '<div class="hbar"></div>' : ''}
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="pv-hint" style="width:${wrapW}px">
      运行的是 <b>package/</b> 真机小游戏包（AppID ${PROJECT.appid}）· 画布 <b>${d.w}×${d.h}</b> 逻辑像素 @${d.dpr}x，
      UI 全部来自 <b>js/main.js + chapter-home.js + achievements.js</b> 的真实 canvas 渲染，非平台侧重绘。<br>
      点左侧任意分镜可直通该节点；广告 / 解锁 / 三轴数值 / 结局页均为真机逻辑。
    </div>`;
  $('#pvReload').onclick = () => { const f = $('#rtFrame'); if (f) f.src = RUNTIME_URL; };
  $('#pvDev').onchange = e => { state.pv.device = e.target.value; renderPreview(); };
  $('#pvScale').onchange = e => { state.pv.scale = +e.target.value; renderPreview(); };
  const fr = $('#rtFrame');
  fr.onload = () => {
    try {
      const doc = fr.contentDocument;
      const s = doc.createElement('script');
      s.textContent = RT_BRIDGE;
      doc.body.appendChild(s);
      state.pv.ready = true;
      if (state.pv.node) setTimeout(() => jumpTo(state.pv.node), 600);
    } catch (err) { console.warn('[preview] bridge inject failed', err); }
  };
}
